Surah: 14. Follow the brief below (surah_map.md) exactly. The evidence is text.md (the surah), dictionary.md (every root of the surah's words, each branch with the classical dictionaries' own phrases), channels.md and hft.md (both are earlier readers' proposals: ignore their judgements (grades, strength or confidence labels, reading types, statements of what a reading may or may not do) and make your own) and your own knowledge of Arabic and the Quran. Return only the map as your final message.

===== _commentary/v16/prompts/r2/surah_map.md =====
Read the surah as one text and write its map of image chains: the lexical images that run through several of
its ayat and join them into one scene, process or movement. A later writer will read one ayah at a time, with
only that ayah's own dictionary. The map lets that writer hear what the ayah's words carry in the surah as a
whole, including senses whose evidence sits under the words of other ayat.

Your evidence is the surah text, the dictionary of every root in the surah (each branch with the classical
dictionaries' own phrases), earlier readers' channel review (channels.md) and activation hypotheses (hft.md),
and your own knowledge of Arabic and the Quran. Where a member or passage comes from memory rather than from
the dictionary or the text, say so. Do not use tools, delegate, browse or inspect files.

The channel review and the HFT records are proposals by earlier readers. Ignore their judgements: grades,
strength or confidence labels, reading types, words such as "surprising", "exploratory" or "latent", and every
statement of what a reading may or may not do. Make your own judgement from the surah's words, the dictionary
phrases and the Quran. Do not rediscover what they already assembled: start from their chains, test each one
against the dictionary phrases and the text, and join, extend, split or correct them. Where their wording
abstracts a member, go back to the dictionary phrase and name what it actually says.

A chain belongs on the map when its members are senses the dictionary attests for words that stand in the
surah, and together they make one image or process that the surah's wording or sequence lets a listener hear.
A member need not be the sense that translates its word in its own ayah; a chain is heard across the surah,
not in one word. Say plainly when a member is a rare sense or is attested only inside a fixed expression, and
keep it. When the dictionary itself joins two of the surah's words in one phrase, quote that phrase: it is
the strongest evidence a chain can have. Keep a scene at the level of its objects, their parts and their
operation. When proposals share members, do not fold one into another's more abstract function unless nothing
concrete is lost. Carry every chain that meets this test, however unusual; leave out proposals that do not.

Write the map in English, with Arabic quoted exactly (surah wording from the text, dictionary phrases from the
dictionary). It is working material for the writer, not commentary prose.

1. `## Chains`. For each chain, a `###` heading naming the image. One paragraph on what the image is and how it
   moves through the surah. Then its members, one line each: the ayah ref, the word as it stands in the text,
   the root and branch id, the dictionary's own phrase quoted exactly, and what this member contributes to the
   image. Add Quran passages outside the surah, with exact refs, where they stage or confirm the chain.
2. `## Ayat`. For each ayah in order: which chains it opens, advances or completes, and what its words add to
   each.
3. `## Not carried`. Each channel subchannel and each HFT record you did not carry into a chain, one line each:
   its name and why.

No ranking and no labels of strength or confidence. No list of what a writer must include. There is no length
target and no required number of chains or members.


===== _commentary/v16/work/s014/surah.r2/text.md =====
# Surah 14

- 14:1 الٓر ۚ كِتَٰبٌ أَنزَلْنَٰهُ إِلَيْكَ لِتُخْرِجَ ٱلنَّاسَ مِنَ ٱلظُّلُمَٰتِ إِلَى ٱلنُّورِ بِإِذْنِ رَبِّهِمْ إِلَىٰ صِرَٰطِ ٱلْعَزِيزِ ٱلْحَمِيدِ
- 14:2 ٱللَّهِ ٱلَّذِى لَهُۥ مَا فِى ٱلسَّمَٰوَٰتِ وَمَا فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ ۗ وَوَيْلٌۭ لِّلْكَٰفِرِينَ مِنْ عَذَابٍۢ شَدِيدٍ
- 14:3 ٱلَّذِينَ يَسْتَحِبُّونَ ٱلْحَيَوٰةَ ٱلدُّنْيَا عَلَى ٱلْءَاخِرَةِ وَيَصُدُّونَ عَن سَبِيلِ ٱللَّهِ وَيَبْغُونَهَا عِوَجًا ۚ أُو۟لَٰٓئِكَ فِى ضَلَٰلٍۭ بَعِيدٍۢ
- 14:4 وَمَآ أَرْسَلْنَا مِن رَّسُولٍ إِلَّا بِلِسَانِ قَوْمِهِۦ لِيُبَيِّنَ لَهُمْ ۖ فَيُضِلُّ ٱللَّهُ مَن يَشَآءُ وَيَهْدِى مَن يَشَآءُ ۚ وَهُوَ ٱلْعَزِيزُ ٱلْحَكِيمُ
- 14:5 وَلَقَدْ أَرْسَلْنَا مُوسَىٰ بِـَٔايَٰتِنَآ أَنْ أَخْرِجْ قَوْمَكَ مِنَ ٱلظُّلُمَٰتِ إِلَى ٱلنُّورِ وَذَكِّرْهُم بِأَيَّىٰمِ ٱللَّهِ ۚ إِنَّ فِى ذَٰلِكَ لَءَايَٰتٍۢ لِّكُلِّ صَبَّارٍۢ شَكُورٍۢ
- 14:6 وَإِذْ قَالَ مُوسَىٰ لِقَوْمِهِ ٱذْكُرُوا۟ نِعْمَةَ ٱللَّهِ عَلَيْكُمْ إِذْ أَنجَىٰكُم مِّنْ ءَالِ فِرْعَوْنَ يَسُومُونَكُمْ سُوٓءَ ٱلْعَذَابِ وَيُذَبِّحُونَ أَبْنَآءَكُمْ وَيَسْتَحْيُونَ نِسَآءَكُمْ ۚ وَفِى ذَٰلِكُم بَلَآءٌۭ مِّن رَّبِّكُمْ عَظِيمٌۭ
- 14:7 وَإِذْ تَأَذَّنَ رَبُّكُمْ لَئِن شَكَرْتُمْ لَأَزِيدَنَّكُمْ ۖ وَلَئِن كَفَرْتُمْ إِنَّ عَذَابِى لَشَدِيدٌۭ
- 14:8 وَقَالَ مُوسَىٰٓ إِن تَكْفُرُوٓا۟ أَنتُمْ وَمَن فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ جَمِيعًۭا فَإِنَّ ٱللَّهَ لَغَنِىٌّ حَمِيدٌ
- 14:9 أَلَمْ يَأْتِكُمْ نَبَؤُا۟ ٱلَّذِينَ مِن قَبْلِكُمْ قَوْمِ نُوحٍۢ وَعَادٍۢ وَثَمُودَ ۛ وَٱلَّذِينَ مِنۢ بَعْدِهِمْ ۛ لَا يَعْلَمُهُمْ إِلَّا ٱللَّهُ ۚ جَآءَتْهُمْ رُسُلُهُم بِٱلْبَيِّنَٰتِ فَرَدُّوٓا۟ أَيْدِيَهُمْ فِىٓ أَفْوَٰهِهِمْ وَقَالُوٓا۟ إِنَّا كَفَرْنَا بِمَآ أُرْسِلْتُم بِهِۦ وَإِنَّا لَفِى شَكٍّۢ مِّمَّا تَدْعُونَنَآ إِلَيْهِ مُرِيبٍۢ
- 14:10 ۞ قَالَتْ رُسُلُهُمْ أَفِى ٱللَّهِ شَكٌّۭ فَاطِرِ ٱلسَّمَٰوَٰتِ وَٱلْأَرْضِ ۖ يَدْعُوكُمْ لِيَغْفِرَ لَكُم مِّن ذُنُوبِكُمْ وَيُؤَخِّرَكُمْ إِلَىٰٓ أَجَلٍۢ مُّسَمًّۭى ۚ قَالُوٓا۟ إِنْ أَنتُمْ إِلَّا بَشَرٌۭ مِّثْلُنَا تُرِيدُونَ أَن تَصُدُّونَا عَمَّا كَانَ يَعْبُدُ ءَابَآؤُنَا فَأْتُونَا بِسُلْطَٰنٍۢ مُّبِينٍۢ
- 14:11 قَالَتْ لَهُمْ رُسُلُهُمْ إِن نَّحْنُ إِلَّا بَشَرٌۭ مِّثْلُكُمْ وَلَٰكِنَّ ٱللَّهَ يَمُنُّ عَلَىٰ مَن يَشَآءُ مِنْ عِبَادِهِۦ ۖ وَمَا كَانَ لَنَآ أَن نَّأْتِيَكُم بِسُلْطَٰنٍ إِلَّا بِإِذْنِ ٱللَّهِ ۚ وَعَلَى ٱللَّهِ فَلْيَتَوَكَّلِ ٱلْمُؤْمِنُونَ
- 14:12 وَمَا لَنَآ أَلَّا نَتَوَكَّلَ عَلَى ٱللَّهِ وَقَدْ هَدَىٰنَا سُبُلَنَا ۚ وَلَنَصْبِرَنَّ عَلَىٰ مَآ ءَاذَيْتُمُونَا ۚ وَعَلَى ٱللَّهِ فَلْيَتَوَكَّلِ ٱلْمُتَوَكِّلُونَ
- 14:13 وَقَالَ ٱلَّذِينَ كَفَرُوا۟ لِرُسُلِهِمْ لَنُخْرِجَنَّكُم مِّنْ أَرْضِنَآ أَوْ لَتَعُودُنَّ فِى مِلَّتِنَا ۖ فَأَوْحَىٰٓ إِلَيْهِمْ رَبُّهُمْ لَنُهْلِكَنَّ ٱلظَّٰلِمِينَ
- 14:14 وَلَنُسْكِنَنَّكُمُ ٱلْأَرْضَ مِنۢ بَعْدِهِمْ ۚ ذَٰلِكَ لِمَنْ خَافَ مَقَامِى وَخَافَ وَعِيدِ
- 14:15 وَٱسْتَفْتَحُوا۟ وَخَابَ كُلُّ جَبَّارٍ عَنِيدٍۢ
- 14:16 مِّن وَرَآئِهِۦ جَهَنَّمُ وَيُسْقَىٰ مِن مَّآءٍۢ صَدِيدٍۢ
- 14:17 يَتَجَرَّعُهُۥ وَلَا يَكَادُ يُسِيغُهُۥ وَيَأْتِيهِ ٱلْمَوْتُ مِن كُلِّ مَكَانٍۢ وَمَا هُوَ بِمَيِّتٍۢ ۖ وَمِن وَرَآئِهِۦ عَذَابٌ غَلِيظٌۭ
- 14:18 مَّثَلُ ٱلَّذِينَ كَفَرُوا۟ بِرَبِّهِمْ ۖ أَعْمَٰلُهُمْ كَرَمَادٍ ٱشْتَدَّتْ بِهِ ٱلرِّيحُ فِى يَوْمٍ عَاصِفٍۢ ۖ لَّا يَقْدِرُونَ مِمَّا كَسَبُوا۟ عَلَىٰ شَىْءٍۢ ۚ ذَٰلِكَ هُوَ ٱلضَّلَٰلُ ٱلْبَعِيدُ
- 14:19 أَلَمْ تَرَ أَنَّ ٱللَّهَ خَلَقَ ٱلسَّمَٰوَٰتِ وَٱلْأَرْضَ بِٱلْحَقِّ ۚ إِن يَشَأْ يُذْهِبْكُمْ وَيَأْتِ بِخَلْقٍۢ جَدِيدٍۢ
- 14:20 وَمَا ذَٰلِكَ عَلَى ٱللَّهِ بِعَزِيزٍۢ
- 14:21 وَبَرَزُوا۟ لِلَّهِ جَمِيعًۭا فَقَالَ ٱلضُّعَفَٰٓؤُا۟ لِلَّذِينَ ٱسْتَكْبَرُوٓا۟ إِنَّا كُنَّا لَكُمْ تَبَعًۭا فَهَلْ أَنتُم مُّغْنُونَ عَنَّا مِنْ عَذَابِ ٱللَّهِ مِن شَىْءٍۢ ۚ قَالُوا۟ لَوْ هَدَىٰنَا ٱللَّهُ لَهَدَيْنَٰكُمْ ۖ سَوَآءٌ عَلَيْنَآ أَجَزِعْنَآ أَمْ صَبَرْنَا مَا لَنَا مِن مَّحِيصٍۢ
- 14:22 وَقَالَ ٱلشَّيْطَٰنُ لَمَّا قُضِىَ ٱلْأَمْرُ إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ وَعَدَكُمْ وَعْدَ ٱلْحَقِّ وَوَعَدتُّكُمْ فَأَخْلَفْتُكُمْ ۖ وَمَا كَانَ لِىَ عَلَيْكُم مِّن سُلْطَٰنٍ إِلَّآ أَن دَعَوْتُكُمْ فَٱسْتَجَبْتُمْ لِى ۖ فَلَا تَلُومُونِى وَلُومُوٓا۟ أَنفُسَكُم ۖ مَّآ أَنَا۠ بِمُصْرِخِكُمْ وَمَآ أَنتُم بِمُصْرِخِىَّ ۖ إِنِّى كَفَرْتُ بِمَآ أَشْرَكْتُمُونِ مِن قَبْلُ ۗ إِنَّ ٱلظَّٰلِمِينَ لَهُمْ عَذَابٌ أَلِيمٌۭ
- 14:23 وَأُدْخِلَ ٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ وَعَمِلُوا۟ ٱلصَّٰلِحَٰتِ جَنَّٰتٍۢ تَجْرِى مِن تَحْتِهَا ٱلْأَنْهَٰرُ خَٰلِدِينَ فِيهَا بِإِذْنِ رَبِّهِمْ ۖ تَحِيَّتُهُمْ فِيهَا سَلَٰمٌ
- 14:24 أَلَمْ تَرَ كَيْفَ ضَرَبَ ٱللَّهُ مَثَلًۭا كَلِمَةًۭ طَيِّبَةًۭ كَشَجَرَةٍۢ طَيِّبَةٍ أَصْلُهَا ثَابِتٌۭ وَفَرْعُهَا فِى ٱلسَّمَآءِ
- 14:25 تُؤْتِىٓ أُكُلَهَا كُلَّ حِينٍۭ بِإِذْنِ رَبِّهَا ۗ وَيَضْرِبُ ٱللَّهُ ٱلْأَمْثَالَ لِلنَّاسِ لَعَلَّهُمْ يَتَذَكَّرُونَ
- 14:26 وَمَثَلُ كَلِمَةٍ خَبِيثَةٍۢ كَشَجَرَةٍ خَبِيثَةٍ ٱجْتُثَّتْ مِن فَوْقِ ٱلْأَرْضِ مَا لَهَا مِن قَرَارٍۢ
- 14:27 يُثَبِّتُ ٱللَّهُ ٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ بِٱلْقَوْلِ ٱلثَّابِتِ فِى ٱلْحَيَوٰةِ ٱلدُّنْيَا وَفِى ٱلْءَاخِرَةِ ۖ وَيُضِلُّ ٱللَّهُ ٱلظَّٰلِمِينَ ۚ وَيَفْعَلُ ٱللَّهُ مَا يَشَآءُ
- 14:28 ۞ أَلَمْ تَرَ إِلَى ٱلَّذِينَ بَدَّلُوا۟ نِعْمَتَ ٱللَّهِ كُفْرًۭا وَأَحَلُّوا۟ قَوْمَهُمْ دَارَ ٱلْبَوَارِ
- 14:29 جَهَنَّمَ يَصْلَوْنَهَا ۖ وَبِئْسَ ٱلْقَرَارُ
- 14:30 وَجَعَلُوا۟ لِلَّهِ أَندَادًۭا لِّيُضِلُّوا۟ عَن سَبِيلِهِۦ ۗ قُلْ تَمَتَّعُوا۟ فَإِنَّ مَصِيرَكُمْ إِلَى ٱلنَّارِ
- 14:31 قُل لِّعِبَادِىَ ٱلَّذِينَ ءَامَنُوا۟ يُقِيمُوا۟ ٱلصَّلَوٰةَ وَيُنفِقُوا۟ مِمَّا رَزَقْنَٰهُمْ سِرًّۭا وَعَلَانِيَةًۭ مِّن قَبْلِ أَن يَأْتِىَ يَوْمٌۭ لَّا بَيْعٌۭ فِيهِ وَلَا خِلَٰلٌ
- 14:32 ٱللَّهُ ٱلَّذِى خَلَقَ ٱلسَّمَٰوَٰتِ وَٱلْأَرْضَ وَأَنزَلَ مِنَ ٱلسَّمَآءِ مَآءًۭ فَأَخْرَجَ بِهِۦ مِنَ ٱلثَّمَرَٰتِ رِزْقًۭا لَّكُمْ ۖ وَسَخَّرَ لَكُمُ ٱلْفُلْكَ لِتَجْرِىَ فِى ٱلْبَحْرِ بِأَمْرِهِۦ ۖ وَسَخَّرَ لَكُمُ ٱلْأَنْهَٰرَ
- 14:33 وَسَخَّرَ لَكُمُ ٱلشَّمْسَ وَٱلْقَمَرَ دَآئِبَيْنِ ۖ وَسَخَّرَ لَكُمُ ٱلَّيْلَ وَٱلنَّهَارَ
- 14:34 وَءَاتَىٰكُم مِّن كُلِّ مَا سَأَلْتُمُوهُ ۚ وَإِن تَعُدُّوا۟ نِعْمَتَ ٱللَّهِ لَا تُحْصُوهَآ ۗ إِنَّ ٱلْإِنسَٰنَ لَظَلُومٌۭ كَفَّارٌۭ
- 14:35 وَإِذْ قَالَ إِبْرَٰهِيمُ رَبِّ ٱجْعَلْ هَٰذَا ٱلْبَلَدَ ءَامِنًۭا وَٱجْنُبْنِى وَبَنِىَّ أَن نَّعْبُدَ ٱلْأَصْنَامَ
- 14:36 رَبِّ إِنَّهُنَّ أَضْلَلْنَ كَثِيرًۭا مِّنَ ٱلنَّاسِ ۖ فَمَن تَبِعَنِى فَإِنَّهُۥ مِنِّى ۖ وَمَنْ عَصَانِى فَإِنَّكَ غَفُورٌۭ رَّحِيمٌۭ
- 14:37 رَّبَّنَآ إِنِّىٓ أَسْكَنتُ مِن ذُرِّيَّتِى بِوَادٍ غَيْرِ ذِى زَرْعٍ عِندَ بَيْتِكَ ٱلْمُحَرَّمِ رَبَّنَا لِيُقِيمُوا۟ ٱلصَّلَوٰةَ فَٱجْعَلْ أَفْـِٔدَةًۭ مِّنَ ٱلنَّاسِ تَهْوِىٓ إِلَيْهِمْ وَٱرْزُقْهُم مِّنَ ٱلثَّمَرَٰتِ لَعَلَّهُمْ يَشْكُرُونَ
- 14:38 رَبَّنَآ إِنَّكَ تَعْلَمُ مَا نُخْفِى وَمَا نُعْلِنُ ۗ وَمَا يَخْفَىٰ عَلَى ٱللَّهِ مِن شَىْءٍۢ فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ وَلَا فِى ٱلسَّمَآءِ
- 14:39 ٱلْحَمْدُ لِلَّهِ ٱلَّذِى وَهَبَ لِى عَلَى ٱلْكِبَرِ إِسْمَٰعِيلَ وَإِسْحَٰقَ ۚ إِنَّ رَبِّى لَسَمِيعُ ٱلدُّعَآءِ
- 14:40 رَبِّ ٱجْعَلْنِى مُقِيمَ ٱلصَّلَوٰةِ وَمِن ذُرِّيَّتِى ۚ رَبَّنَا وَتَقَبَّلْ دُعَآءِ
- 14:41 رَبَّنَا ٱغْفِرْ لِى وَلِوَٰلِدَىَّ وَلِلْمُؤْمِنِينَ يَوْمَ يَقُومُ ٱلْحِسَابُ
- 14:42 وَلَا تَحْسَبَنَّ ٱللَّهَ غَٰفِلًا عَمَّا يَعْمَلُ ٱلظَّٰلِمُونَ ۚ إِنَّمَا يُؤَخِّرُهُمْ لِيَوْمٍۢ تَشْخَصُ فِيهِ ٱلْأَبْصَٰرُ
- 14:43 مُهْطِعِينَ مُقْنِعِى رُءُوسِهِمْ لَا يَرْتَدُّ إِلَيْهِمْ طَرْفُهُمْ ۖ وَأَفْـِٔدَتُهُمْ هَوَآءٌۭ
- 14:44 وَأَنذِرِ ٱلنَّاسَ يَوْمَ يَأْتِيهِمُ ٱلْعَذَابُ فَيَقُولُ ٱلَّذِينَ ظَلَمُوا۟ رَبَّنَآ أَخِّرْنَآ إِلَىٰٓ أَجَلٍۢ قَرِيبٍۢ نُّجِبْ دَعْوَتَكَ وَنَتَّبِعِ ٱلرُّسُلَ ۗ أَوَلَمْ تَكُونُوٓا۟ أَقْسَمْتُم مِّن قَبْلُ مَا لَكُم مِّن زَوَالٍۢ
- 14:45 وَسَكَنتُمْ فِى مَسَٰكِنِ ٱلَّذِينَ ظَلَمُوٓا۟ أَنفُسَهُمْ وَتَبَيَّنَ لَكُمْ كَيْفَ فَعَلْنَا بِهِمْ وَضَرَبْنَا لَكُمُ ٱلْأَمْثَالَ
- 14:46 وَقَدْ مَكَرُوا۟ مَكْرَهُمْ وَعِندَ ٱللَّهِ مَكْرُهُمْ وَإِن كَانَ مَكْرُهُمْ لِتَزُولَ مِنْهُ ٱلْجِبَالُ
- 14:47 فَلَا تَحْسَبَنَّ ٱللَّهَ مُخْلِفَ وَعْدِهِۦ رُسُلَهُۥٓ ۗ إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ عَزِيزٌۭ ذُو ٱنتِقَامٍۢ
- 14:48 يَوْمَ تُبَدَّلُ ٱلْأَرْضُ غَيْرَ ٱلْأَرْضِ وَٱلسَّمَٰوَٰتُ ۖ وَبَرَزُوا۟ لِلَّهِ ٱلْوَٰحِدِ ٱلْقَهَّارِ
- 14:49 وَتَرَى ٱلْمُجْرِمِينَ يَوْمَئِذٍۢ مُّقَرَّنِينَ فِى ٱلْأَصْفَادِ
- 14:50 سَرَابِيلُهُم مِّن قَطِرَانٍۢ وَتَغْشَىٰ وُجُوهَهُمُ ٱلنَّارُ
- 14:51 لِيَجْزِىَ ٱللَّهُ كُلَّ نَفْسٍۢ مَّا كَسَبَتْ ۚ إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ سَرِيعُ ٱلْحِسَابِ
- 14:52 هَٰذَا بَلَٰغٌۭ لِّلنَّاسِ وَلِيُنذَرُوا۟ بِهِۦ وَلِيَعْلَمُوٓا۟ أَنَّمَا هُوَ إِلَٰهٌۭ وَٰحِدٌۭ وَلِيَذَّكَّرَ أُو۟لُوا۟ ٱلْأَلْبَٰبِ


===== _commentary/v16/work/s014/surah.r2/dictionary.md =====
# Dictionary: every branch of every focus root

Every root of the surah's words, each once, headed by the ayah and word where it first occurs; it also serves the later words listed with it.
One entry per branch: a Turkish label and Turkish glosses of its attested senses, then the classical dictionaries' own phrases with their source tags (ayn, sihah, tahdhib, maqayis, mufradat, jamhara). [kalıp] marks a sense the dictionaries attest only inside a fixed expression.
Identity roots come from the quran-data gateway. Word-scoped alternatives are cited analyses of this
exact word. **Echo roots** are observed but withheld mappings: sound-family candidates, not identity.

## ك ت ب (root_001283): 14:1 كِتَٰبٌ

- **B001** bir şeyi başka bir şeye katıp birleştirme — bir şeyi başka bir şeye katıp birleştirme · su tulumunu dikerek birleştirmek · katırın üreme organının dudaklarını halka veya kayışla birleştirmek · dişi devenin burun deliklerini iplikle dikmek veya bağlamak · dişi devenin memelerini bağlamak · su tulumunun ağzını bağıyla sıkıca kapatmak · kayışın iki yüzünü birleştiren boncuk · bir arada duran atlı veya askerî birlik · atların toplanması · askerleri birlik birlik düzenlemek
  أصل صحيح واحد يدل على جمع شيء إلى شيء (maqayis)؛ أصل الكتب ضمك الشيء إلى الشيء (jamhara)؛ ضم أديم إلى أديم بالخياطة (mufradat)؛ كتبت السقاء إذا خرزته (tahdhib)؛ كتبت البغلة إذا جمعت بين شفريها بحلقة (sihah;mufradat)؛ الكتيبة جماعة مستحيزة (sihah;tahdhib)
- **B002** yazma ve yazılı metin — kitabı yazmak veya kopyalamak · yazılı metin veya üzerinde yazı bulunan sayfa · yazma işi ve yazıcılık · kitabı yazmak veya kopyalamak · ona şiiri söyleyerek yazdırmak · birinden kendisi için bir şey yazmasını istemek · çocuğa yazmayı öğretmek · yazı öğretmeni veya yazı öğretilen yer · öğretim yerindeki çocuklar veya onların topluluğu
  الكتاب والكتابة يقال كتبت الكتاب أكتبه كتبا (maqayis)؛ وقد كتب الكتاب يكتبه كتبا إذا جمع حروفه (jamhara)؛ الكتاب معروف وقد كتبت كتبا وكتابا وكتابة (sihah)؛ كتبت الكتاب كتبا وكتابا فالكتاب اسم لما كتب مجموعا (tahdhib)؛ في التعارف ضم الحروف بعضها إلى بعض بالخط (mufradat)؛ أكتبني هذه القصيدة أي أملها علي (sihah)؛ استكتبه الشيء أي سأله أن يكتبه له (sihah;tahdhib)
- **B003** bağlayıcı olarak hükme bağlama ve belirleme — yükümlülük, hüküm veya yazgı · size zorunlu kılındı · Tanrı belirledi, karara bağladı veya zorunlu kıldı
  الكتاب وهو الفرض (maqayis)؛ يقال للحكم الكتاب (maqayis)؛ يقال للقدر الكتاب (maqayis)؛ الكتاب الفرض والحكم والقدر (sihah)؛ الكتاب يوضع موضع الفرض (tahdhib)؛ يعبر عن الإثبات والتقدير والإيجاب والفرض والعزم بالكتابة (mufradat)؛ يعبر بالكتابة عن القضاء الممضى (mufradat)
- **B004** adını sicile yazma veya bir gruba dâhil etme — pay veya geçim tahsisatı için kaydolma · adını pay kaydına veya yönetim siciline yazdırmak · bizi tanıklar topluluğuna kat
  الكتبة الاكتتاب في الفرض والرزق (ayn;tahdhib)؛ اكتتب فلان أي كتب اسمه في الفرض (ayn;tahdhib)؛ اكتتب الرجل إذا كتب نفسه في ديوان السلطان (sihah)؛ فاكتبنا مع الشاهدين أي اجعلنا في زمرتهم (mufradat)
- **B005** özgürlük bedelini ödemeye dayalı özgürleşme sözleşmesi — kölenin bedelini ödeyerek özgürlüğünü kazanma sözleşmesi · özgürlük bedeli sözleşmesinin tarafı olan köle; bağlama göre sahibi · köleyle özgürlük bedeli ödemesine dayalı sözleşme yapmak · kölenin özgürlüğünü satın almak için yaptığı sözleşme
  المكاتب العبد يكاتبه سيده على نفسه (maqayis)؛ المكاتب الذي يشتري نفسه ويكاتب عليها (jamhara)؛ المكاتب العبد يكاتب على نفسه بثمنه فإذا سعى وأداه عتق (sihah)؛ معنى الكتاب والمكاتبة أن يكاتب الرجل عبده أو أمته على مال ينجمه عليه (tahdhib)؛ كتابة العبد ابتياع نفسه من سيده بما يؤديه من كسبه (mufradat)

## ن ز ل (root_001492): 14:1 أَنزَلْنَٰهُ, 14:32 وَأَنزَلَ

- **B001** aşağı inme veya bir yere konaklama — yüksekten inmek veya bir yerde konaklamak · yağmurun gökten yağması · bir yerde yükünü bırakıp konaklamak · ağır ağır inme
  هبوط شيء ووقوعه؛ نزل عن دابته نزولا؛ نزل المطر من السماء نزولا (maqayis)؛ نزل فلان عن الدابة أو من علو إلى سفل (ayn)؛ المنزل النزول وهو الحلول؛ التنزل النزول في مهلة (sihah)؛ النزول في الأصل هو انحطاط من علو؛ نزل في مكان كذا حط رحله (mufradat)
- **B002** Tanrısal iyilik, ceza veya bildiriyi insanlara ulaştırma — başkasını indirmek veya bir şeyi yerine ulaştırmak · Tanrı'nın iyilikleri ve cezaları insanlara vermesi · bölüm bölüm ve yinelenerek bildirme · Tanrısal esirgemenin onlara erişmesi
  تنزلت الرحمة عليهم (tahdhib)؛ إنزال الله تعالى نعمه ونقمه على الخلق وإعطاؤهم إياها؛ إما بإنزال الشيء نفسه كإنزال القرآن وإما بإنزال أسبابه؛ التنزيل يختص بما أنزل مفرقا ومرة بعد أخرى (mufradat)
- **B003** konaklama yeri veya bulunulan derece — konaklama yeri, ev veya su başı · derece veya konum · birinin derecesini düşürmek · topluluğu konaklama yerlerine yerleştirmek
  مكان نزل ينزل فيه كثيرا؛ وجدت القوم على نزلاتهم أي منازلهم (maqayis)؛ المنزل المنهل والدار؛ المنزلة المرتبة؛ استنزل فلان أي حط عن مرتبته (sihah)؛ نزلت القوم أي أنزلتهم المنازل؛ نزل فلان غيره أي قدر لها المنازل (tahdhib)؛ أنزلني منزلا مباركا؛ نزل في مكان كذا حط رحله (mufradat)
- **B004** bir şeyi uygun yerine veya sırasına koyma — bir şeyi düzenleyip uygun yerine veya sırasına koyma
  التنزيل ترتيب الشيء ووضعه منزله (maqayis)؛ التنزيل أيضا الترتيب (sihah)؛ التنزيل يختص بالموضع الذي يشير إليه إنزاله مفرقا ومرة بعد أخرى (mufradat)
- **B005** konuk için hazırlanan yiyecek ve ağırlama payı — konuğa hazırlanan yiyecek veya yol azığı · ürün geliri, fazlalık veya bağış · konuk · birini konuk etmek · topluluğun geçim payları · bol ve bereketli yemek · bol veren ve eli açık olan · bir araya gelmiş pay
  النزل ما يهيأ للنزيل؛ طعام ذو نزل؛ النزيل الضيف (maqayis)؛ النزل ما يهيأ للقوم والضيف؛ النزل ريع ما يزرع (ayn)؛ النزل ما يهيأ للنزيل؛ النزل أيضا الريع؛ النزيل الضيف (sihah)؛ حسن النزل أي الضيافة؛ أنزال القوم أرزاقهم؛ أقمت لهم غذاءهم وما يصلح معه أن ينزلوا عليه؛ النزل الريع والفضل (tahdhib)؛ النزل ما يعد للنازل من الزاد؛ أنزلت فلانا أضفته؛ ذو نزل له ريع؛ حظ نزل مجتمع تشبيها بالطعام النزل (mufradat)
- **B006** başa gelen ağır sıkıntı — insanların başına gelen ağır sıkıntı veya felaket
  النازلة الشديدة من شدائد الدهر تنزل (maqayis)؛ النازلة الشديدة من شدائد الدهر تنزل بالقوم وجمعها النوازل (ayn)؛ النازلة الشديدة من شدائد الدهر تنزل بالناس (sihah;tahdhib)؛ يعبر بالنازلة عن الشدة وجمعها نوازل (mufradat)
- **B007** savaşmak için karşı karşıya inme — savaşta karşı karşıya gelme · savaşmak üzere inin
  النزال في الحرب أن يتنازل الفريقان؛ نزال كلمة توضع موضع انزل (maqayis)؛ النزال المنازلة في الحرب أن ينزلا معا فيقتتلا؛ نزال أي انزلوا للحرب (ayn)؛ نزال بمعنى انزل؛ النزال في الحرب أن يتنازل الفريقان (sihah)؛ النزال في الحرب المنازلة (mufradat)
- **B008** hac yolculuğunda Mina'ya varma — hac yapmak veya Mina'ya gelmek · topluluğun Mina'ya gelmesi
  يعبرون عن الحج بالنزول؛ نزل إذا حج؛ نزلنا أتينا منى (maqayis)؛ نزل القوم إذا أتوا منى (sihah;tahdhib)؛ نزل فلان إذا أتى منى (mufradat)
- **B009** erkeğin dışarı çıkan üreme sıvısı — erkeğin dışarı çıkan üreme sıvısı · cinsel birleşme sırasında boşalmak · kadının erkeğin boşalmasını istemesi
  النزالة ماء الرجل (maqayis)؛ النزالة بالضم ماء الرجل وقد أنزل (sihah)؛ أنزل الرجل ماءه إذا جامع والمرأة تستنزل ذلك (tahdhib)؛ النزالة والنزل يكنى بهما عن ماء الرجل إذا خرج عنه (mufradat)
- **B010** bir kez inme — bir kez inme · bir kez daha · soğuk algınlığına benzer geçici rahatsızlık
  النزلة المرة الواحدة؛ ولقد رآه نزلة أخرى أي مرة أخرى (ayn)؛ النزلة كالزكام؛ ولقد رآه نزلة أخرى قالوا مرة أخرى (sihah)؛ النزلة المرة الواحدة من النزول (tahdhib)
- **B011** akış, konaklanma veya biçim özelliğiyle nitelenen yer [kalıp] — az yağmurda bile hızla su akıtan sert arazi · sık konaklanan, geniş uzak, otlaklı veya suyu çabuk akan yer · dar vadi
  مكان نزل ينزل فيه كثيرا (maqayis)؛ أرض نزلة ومكان نزل إذا كانت تسيل من أدنى مطر لصلابتها (sihah)؛ طعام نزل وأرض نزلة ومكان نزل سريع السيل؛ مكان نزل ينزل فيه كثيرا؛ مكان نزل واسع بعيد؛ مكان نزل إذا كان محلالا مربا؛ النزل من الأودية الضيق منها (tahdhib)

## خ ر ج (root_000400): 14:1 لِتُخْرِجَ, 14:5 أَخْرِجْ, 14:13 لَنُخْرِجَنَّكُم, 14:32 فَأَخْرَجَ

- **B001** bir yerden ya da durumdan dışarı çıkma — dışarı çıktı; bir yerden veya durumdan ayrıldı · dışarı çıkma; bir durumdan ayrılma · dışarı çıkan veya ayrılan · çıkış yeri veya çıkış yönü
  النفاذ عن الشيء (maqayis)؛ الخروج نقيض الدخول (ayn;jamhara;tahdhib)؛ خرج خروجا برز من مقره أو حاله (mufradat)
- **B002** bir şeyi çıkarma, elde etme veya yetiştirme — dışarı çıkardı veya ortaya koydu · nesneleri dışarı çıkarma veya görünür kılma · çıkarıp elde etti · işleyip ortaya çıkarma veya çeşitlere ayırma · eğitim görüp yetişti · birinin elinde yetişmiş öğrenci
  اخترجت الرجل واستخرجته سواء (ayn)؛ الاستخراج كالاستنباط (sihah)؛ الإخراج أكثر ما يقال في الأعيان (mufradat)؛ خريج فلان كأنه أخرجه من حد الجهل (maqayis)
- **B003** düzenli mali yükümlülük, getiri veya gider — mali ödeme, vergi veya ürün getirisi · ürün getirisi, vergi veya zorunlu ödeme · hizmetindeki kişiyle aylık ödeme üzerinde anlaştı · efendisine düzenli ödeme yapmakla yükümlü köle · sorumluluk karşılığında elde edilen ürün getirisi
  الخراج والخرج الإتاوة لأنه مال يخرجه المعطي (maqayis)؛ الخرج والخراج ما يخرج من المال في السنة بقدر معلوم (ayn;tahdhib)؛ الخراج الغلة (tahdhib)؛ الخرج بإزاء الدخل (mufradat)
- **B004** bedende çıkan irinli şişlik veya yara — bedende çıkan şişlik, çıban veya irinli yara

  أصل مطرد منقاس وهو الولوج (maqayis)؛ دخل يدخل دخولا (maqayis)؛ ادخل في غار وتدخل فيه (ayn)؛ دخلت الدار وغيرها وأدخلت غيري (jamhara)؛ دخلت البيت وادخل وتدخل الشيء (sihah)؛ الدخول نقيض الخروج ويستعمل في المكان والزمان والأعمال (mufradat)
- **B002** eşiyle cinsel birleşmede bulunmak [kalıp] — eşiyle cinsel birleşmede bulunmak
  دخل بامرأته كناية عن الإفضاء إليها (mufradat)
- **B003** içte kalan yan — bir işin veya kişinin iç yüzü · giysinin bedene bakan iç kenarı · saklı tutulan iş veya açılan gizli iç yüz
  الدخلة باطن أمر الرجل وأنا عالم بدخلته (maqayis)؛ الدخلة بطانة من الأمر وعالم بدخلة أمرهم (ayn)؛ دخلل أمري إذا بثثته مكتومك (jamhara)؛ داخلة الإزار طرفه الذي يلي الجسد وداخلة الرجل باطن أمره (sihah)
- **B004** içten bozan kusur — içteki kusur, bozukluk veya kuşku · antları hile ve aldatma aracı yapmak · içten kusurlu, zayıf veya zihni bozuk · içi çürümüş palmiye · böcekçe yenmiş veya kurtlanmış yiyecek
  الدخل العيب في الحسب وكالدغل (maqayis)؛ دخل فلان وهو مدخول إذا كان في عقله دخل ونخلة مدخولة عفنة الجوف (maqayis)؛ عيب في الحسب وفي هذا الأمر دخل ودغل ودخل حسبه أو عقله (ayn)؛ في أمره دخل أي فساد (jamhara)؛ الدخل العيب والريبة ومكرا وخديعة ومدخول في عقله ونخلة مدخولة (sihah)؛ الدخل كناية عن الفساد والعداوة المستبطنة كالدغل ومدخول كناية عن بله في عقله وفساد في أصله (mufradat)
- **B005** sonradan araya katılan kimse — özel işlere alınan veya bir topluluğa dışarıdan katılan kimse · kişinin özel işlerine aldığı yakın kimse · bilmediği işlere zorla karışan kimse
  دخيلك الذي يداخلك في أمورك وبنو فلان في بني فلان دخيل (maqayis)؛ دخيلك الذي تدخله في أمورك ودخلل والمتدخل في الأمور المتكلف فيها (ayn)؛ فلان دخيل في بني فلان إذا كان من غيرهم (jamhara)؛ هم دخل في بني فلان ودخيل الرجل ودخلله الذي يداخله في أموره (sihah)؛ وعن الدعوة في النسب (mufradat)
- **B006** gelir — gelir veya içeri giren kazanç
  الدخل ما دخل ضيعة الإنسان من المنالة (ayn)؛ الدخل خلاف الخرج (sihah)
- **B007** develeri yeniden ya da araya katarak sulama — develeri ikinci kez suya götürme veya susuz deveyi sürüye katma
  الدخال في الورد أن تشرب الإبل ثم ترد إلى الحوض (maqayis)؛ سقيت الإبل دخالا إذا حملتها على الحوض ثانية والدخال في وجه آخر أن تحملها على الحوض بمرة واحدة عراكا (ayn)؛ أورد الرجل إبله دخالا (jamhara)؛ الدخال في الورد أن يشرب البعير ثم يرد من العطن إلى الحوض (sihah)؛ الدخال في الإبل أن يدخل إبل في أثناء ما لم تشرب لتشرب معها ثانيا (mufradat)
- **B008** iç içe geçme ve arada kalma — eklemlerin birbirine geçmesi · bir sinir üzerinde toplanmış et parçası · bir ana renge karışmış başka renkler · kuşun sırtıyla karnı arasındaki tüyler · ağaç köklerinin arasına girmiş ot
  كل لحمة مجتمعة دخلة والدخل من ريش الطائر ما بين الظهران والبطنان والدخل من الكلأ ما دخل منه في أصول الشجر (maqayis)؛ الدخلة في اللون تحليط من ألوان في لون والدخال مداخلة المفاصل بعضها في بعض (ayn)؛ كل لحمة مجتمعة على عصب فهي دخلة (jamhara)؛ الدخل من الكلأ ما دخل منه في أصول الشجر (sihah)
- **B009** sık ağaçlıkta barınan küçük kuş — oyuklarda ve sık ağaç altında barınan küçük kuş · bu küçük kuş adının bir çoğul biçimi · bu küçük kuş adının öteki çoğul biçimi
  بذلك سمي هذا الطائر دخلا (maqayis)؛ الدخل صغار الطير مأواها الغيران وبطون الأودية تحت شجر ملتف والجميع الدخاخيل (ayn)؛ الدخل طائر صغير وجمع دخل دخاخيل (jamhara)؛ الدخل طائر صغير والجمع الدخاليل (sihah)؛ الدخل طائر سمي بذلك لدخوله فيما بين الأشجار الملتفة (mufradat)
- **B010** taze palmiye meyvesi için küçük örgü sepet — taze palmiye meyvesi konan küçük örgü sepet
  الدوخلة سفيفة من خوص صغيرة يجعل فيها الرطب (ayn)؛ الدوخلة هذا المنسوج من الخوص يجعل فيه الرطب (sihah)؛ الدوخلة معروفة (mufradat)

## ص ل ح (root_000876): 14:23 ٱلصَّٰلِحَٰتِ

- **B001** iyi ve düzgün olma; düzeltme — iyilik, düzgünlük ve bozulmamışlık · iyi ve düzgün duruma gelmek · kendisi iyi ve düzgün olan kişi; iyi ve yararlı iş · işlerini düzelten veya başkasını iyi duruma getiren kimse · bozukluğu giderip iyi ve düzgün duruma getirme · hayvana iyi davranmak · iyilik ya da yarar sağlayan şey · iyi ve yararlı duruma getirmeye çalışma
  أصل واحد يدل على خلاف الفساد (maqayis)؛ الصلاح نقيض الطلاح ورجل صالح ومصلح وأصلحت إلى الدابة أحسنت إليها (ayn)؛ الصلاح ضد الطلاح وصلح الرجل صلاحا وصلوحا (jamhara)؛ الصلاح ضد الفساد والاصلاح نقيض الإفساد والمصلحة والاستصلاح (sihah)؛ الصلاح ضد الفساد مختصان في أكثر الاستعمال بالأفعال وإصلاح الله تعالى الإنسان (mufradat)
- **B002** barışma ve uzlaşma — barışma, uzlaşma ve aradaki soğukluğun giderilmesi · birbiriyle barışıp uzlaşmak
  والصلح تصالح القوم بينهم (ayn)؛ الصلاح بكسر الصاد المصالحة والاسم الصلح وقد اصطلحا وتصالحا واصالحا (sihah)؛ الصلح يختص بإزالة النفار بين الناس، يقال اصطلحوا وتصالحوا (mufradat)
- **B003** sana uygun olma [kalıp] — bu sana uygundur; bu sana uyar
  وهذا الشئ يصلح لك، أي هو من بابتك (sihah)
- **B004** kişi adı olan kök türevleri — bu kökten türemiş kişi adları; bunlardan biri bir peygamber adıdır
  وقد سمت العرب صالحا وصليحا ومصلحا (jamhara)؛ وصالح اسم للنبي عليه السلام (mufradat)
- **B005** bir kent ve bir nehir için özel adlar — belirli bir kentin özel adı · belirli bir nehrin özel adı
  إن مكة تسمى صلاحا (maqayis)؛ والصلح نهر بميسان (ayn)؛ وصلاح في وزن حذام وقطام وهو اسم مكة (jamhara)؛ وصلاح مثل قطام اسم مكة (sihah)

## ج ن ن (root_000266): 14:23 جَنَّٰتٍ

- **B001** örtme ve duyulardan gizleme — örtmek; gizleyecek bir örtü sağlamak; içinde saklamak · bir şeyin arkasına gizlenmek · insanı örten giysi veya örtü
  الجيم والنون أصل واحد وهو الستر والتستر (maqayis)؛ أصل الجن ستر الشيء عن الحاسة (mufradat)؛ استجن فلان إذا استتر بشيء (ayn;tahdhib)؛ أجننت الشيء في صدري أكننته (sihah)؛ ما علي جنان إلا ما ترى أي ثوب يواريني (sihah;tahdhib)
- **B002** gecenin karartıp örtmesi — gecenin kararıp üzerini örtmesi · gecenin koyu karanlığı ve nesneleri örtmesi
  جنان الليل سواده وستره الأشياء (maqayis)؛ أجنه الليل وجن عليه الليل إذا أظلم حتى يستره بظلمته (ayn)؛ جن عليه الليل يجن بالضم جنونا (sihah)؛ جن عليه الليل وأجنه الليل إذا أظلم حتى يستره بظلمته (tahdhib)؛ جنه الليل وأجنه وجن عليه (mufradat)
- **B003** zemini ağaçlarla örtülü bahçe — zemini ağaçlarla örtülü bahçe veya koruluk
  الجنة البستان (maqayis;sihah)؛ الجنة الحديقة وهي بستان ذات شجر ونزهة (ayn)؛ العرب تسمي النخيل جنة (sihah)؛ كل بستان ذي شجر يستر بأشجاره الأرض (mufradat)
- **B004** ölüm sonrası gizli nimetler yurdu — ölüm sonrası ödül ve gizli nimetler yurdu
  الجنة ما يصير إليه المسلمون في الآخرة وهو ثواب مستور عنهم اليوم (maqayis)؛ سميت الجنة إما تشبيها بالجنة في الأرض وإما لستره نعمها عنا (mufradat)
- **B005** gözle görülmeyen ruhani varlıklar topluluğu — gözle görülmeyen ruhani varlıklar · görünmeyen varlıkların atası veya bir bireyi · görünmeyen ruhani varlıkların topluluğu · görünmeyen ruhani varlıkların çok bulunduğu yer
  الجن سموا بذلك لأنهم متسترون عن أعين الخلق (maqayis)؛ الجن جماعة ولد الجان وجمعهم الجنة والجنان (ayn;tahdhib)؛ الجن خلاف الإنس والواحد جني (sihah)؛ الجنة جماعة الجن (mufradat)؛ أرض مجنة كثيرة الجن (ayn;sihah;tahdhib)
- **B006** aklı örten akıl yitimi — aklını yitirmek; aklını yitirmiş duruma getirmek · akıl yitimi; benlik ile akıl arasındaki engel · aklını yitirmiş gibi davranmak
  الجنة الجنون وذلك أنه يغطي العقل (maqayis)؛ المجنة الجنون وجن الرجل وأجنه الله فهو مجنون (ayn)؛ جن الرجل جنونا وأجنه الله فهو مجنون (sihah)؛ به جنون وجنة ومجنة (tahdhib)؛ الجنون حائل بين النفس والعقل (mufradat)
- **B007** ana rahmindeki doğmamış çocuk — ana rahmindeki doğmamış çocuk · rahminde çocuk taşımak; çocuğun rahimde saklı kalması
  الجنين الولد في بطن أمه (maqayis)؛ أجنت الحامل الجنين أي الولد في بطنها (ayn)؛ الجنين الولد ما دام في البطن (sihah)؛ الجنين الولد في الرحم (tahdhib)؛ الجنين الولد ما دام في بطن أمه (mufradat)
- **B008** koruyucu siper veya savaş donanımı — koruyucu örtü, siper veya savaş donanımı · kalkan
  المجن الترس وكل ما استتر به من السلاح فهو جنة (maqayis)؛ المجن الترس والجنة الدرع وكل ما وقاك فهو جنتك (ayn)؛ الجنة ما استترت به من سلاح والجنة السترة والمجن الترس (sihah)؛ المجن الترس (tahdhib)؛ المجن والمجنة الترس الذي يجن صاحبه (mufradat)
- **B009** ölüyü örtüp gömme — ölüyü örtmek ve gömmek · gömüt; ölü örtüsü; gömülmüş kişi
  الجنين المقبور (maqayis)؛ الجنن القبر وقيل للكفن أيضا (ayn)؛ جننت الميت وأجننته أي واريته والجنن القبر (sihah)؛ جننته في القبر وأجننته والجنن القبر والجنن الكفن (tahdhib)؛ الجنين القبر (mufradat)
- **B010** duyulardan saklı yürek ve gizli yön — yürek veya yüreğin saklı iç yönü · gizli iş veya görünmeyen yön
  الجنان القلب (maqayis)؛ الجنان روع القلب (ayn;tahdhib)؛ أراد بالجن القلب (sihah)؛ الجنان القلب لكونه مستورا عن الحاسة (mufradat)؛ الجنان الأمر الخفي (tahdhib)
- **B011** bitkinin güçlenip boylanması ve sıklaşması — bitkinin güçlenmesi, boylanması, sıklaşması veya çiçek açması · uzun ağaç; bol otlu ve henüz otlanmamış arazi
  جن النبت جنونا إذا اشتد وخرج زهره (maqayis)؛ جن النبت جنونا أي طال والتف وخرج زهره ونخلة مجنونة أي طويلة (sihah)؛ للنبت الملتف الكثيف مجنون وجنت الرياض جنونا إذا اعتم نبتها (tahdhib)؛ جن التلاع والآفاق أي كثر عشبها (mufradat)
- **B012** yılan, özellikle beyaz bir tür — yılan, beyaz yılan veya belirli bir yılan türü
  الحية الذي يسمى الجان فهو تشبيه له بالواحد من الجان (maqayis)؛ الجان حية بيضاء (ayn)؛ الجان أيضا حية بيضاء (sihah)؛ الجان الحية وجمعها جوان (tahdhib)؛ الجان ضرب من الحيات (mufradat)
- **B013** halkın büyük kitlesi — insanların çoğunluğu veya halkın büyük kitlesi
  جنان الناس معظمهم ويسمى السواد (maqayis)؛ جنان الناس دهماؤهم (sihah)؛ جنانهم جماعتهم وسوادهم (tahdhib)
- **B014** bir şeyin ilk ve yeni dönemi — gençliğin, çocukluğun veya bir dönemin ilk başlangıcı
  كان ذلك في جن شبابه أي في أول شبابه (sihah)؛ كان ذلك في جن صباه أي في حداثته وكذلك جن كل شيء أول ابتدائه (tahdhib)
- **B015** uçuş sırasında çoğalan sinek vızıltısı — sineğin vızıltısının veya sesinin çoğalması · böcekse uçuş vızıltısının artması; bitkiyse sıklaşıp dolaşması
  جن الذباب أي كثر صوته (sihah)؛ جن الخازباز به جنونا يحتمل هذين الوجهين (sihah)؛ قيل هو ذباب وجنونه كثرة ترنمه في طيرانه وقيل هو نبت وجنون النبت التفافه (tahdhib)
- **B016** göğüs kemikleri ve kaburga uçları — göğüs kemikleri veya kaburgaların göğse yakın uçları
  الجناجن عظام الصدر (maqayis)؛ الجنجن والجناجن أطراف الأضلاع مما يلي الصدر وعظم القلب (ayn)؛ الجناجن عظام الصدر الواحد جنجن (sihah)
- **B017** içine girilip saklanılan yer — saklanılan yer; ayrıca kaynakta belirli bir eski pazar yerinin adı
  المجنة اسم موضع على أميال من مكة؛ كانت مجنة وذو المجاز وعكاظ أسواقا في الجاهلية؛ المجنة أيضا الموضع الذي يستتر فيه (sihah)

## ج ر ي (root_000240): 14:23 تَجْرِى, 14:32 لِتَجْرِىَ

- **B001** bir yol boyunca akıp, koşup ya da ilerleyerek gitme — hızlı ilerleyiş ve kendi yatağında akış · aktı, koştu ya da yol aldı · suyun akışı · at koşusu · akıtmak ya da harekete geçirmek · akış, gidiş yolu ya da koşu yeri · denizde yol alan gemi · gökte yol alan güneş · suyu akan pınar · denizde yol alan gemiler · çeşitli koşu biçimleri olan at · kötücül ayartıcının sizi kendi işi doğrultusunda sürüklemesine ya da kendine aracı kılmasına izin vermeyin
  أصل واحد وهو انسياح الشيء (maqayis)؛ جرى الماء يجري جرية وجريا وجريانا (maqayis;sihah;mufradat)؛ الخيل تجري والرياح تجري والشمس تجري جريا (ayn;tahdhib)؛ الجارية السفينة والجارية الشمس (maqayis;sihah)
- **B002** alışılmış yol ve davranış düzeni — kişinin alışkanlık edindiği ve sürekli izlediği yol
  للعادة الإجريا (maqayis)؛ الإجريا طريقته التي يجري عليها من عادته (ayn)؛ الإجريا الجري والعادة مما تأخذ فيه (sihah)؛ الإجرياء الوجه الذي نأخذ فيه (tahdhib)؛ الإجريا العادة التي يجري عليها الإنسان (mufradat)
- **B003** başkası adına iş gören, haber götüren ya da güvence veren kimse — başkası adına iş gören, haber götüren ya da güvence veren kimse · başkası adına iş görecek birini tutmak · kötücül ayartıcının sizi kendi işi doğrultusunda sürüklemesine ya da kendine aracı kılmasına izin vermeyin
  الجري الوكيل (maqayis;tahdhib)؛ الجري الرسول (ayn;tahdhib;mufradat)؛ الجري الضامن (tahdhib)؛ استجريت أي اتخذت وكيلا (maqayis;sihah;tahdhib)؛ لا يستجرينكم الشيطان (maqayis;sihah;tahdhib;mufradat)
- **B004** genç kız ve ona bağlı genç kızlık çağı — genç kız ya da hizmette çalıştırılan genç kadın · genç kızlık çağı · genç kızlık durumu
  الجارية من النساء لأنها تستجرى في الخدمة (maqayis)؛ الجارية مصدرها الجراء (ayn)؛ جارية بينة الجراية والجراء (sihah;tahdhib)؛ أيام جرائها أي صباها (maqayis;ayn;sihah)
- **B005** kuş kursağı — 
  الجرية وهي الحوصلة أصلها قرية (maqayis)؛ الجرية مثل القرية هي الحوصلة (sihah)؛ الجرية والقرية والنوطة لحوصلة الطائر (tahdhib)؛ يقال للحوصلة جرية لأنها مجرى الطعام (mufradat)
- **B006** sürekli verilen geçimlik ya da kalıcı yarar — düzenli görev ödeneği · onun için sürekli verildi ya da sürüp gitti · ona sürekli olarak verdim · yararı süren bağış
  الجراية الجاري من الوظائف (sihah)؛ الأرزاق جارية والأعطيات دارة (tahdhib)؛ جرى عليه ذلك الشيء ودر له بمعنى دام له (tahdhib)؛ أجريت له كذا أي أدمت له (tahdhib)؛ صدقة جارية (tahdhib)
- **B007** birlikte ilerleyip birbirine ayak uydurma — yanında koşmak ya da ona ayak uydurmak · söyleşide ona ayak uydurmak ve karşılık vermek
  جاراه مجاراة وجراء أي جرى معه (sihah)؛ جاراه في الحديث وتجاروا فيه (sihah)
- **B008** senin yüzünden ya da senin için — senin yüzünden ya da senin için
  فعلت ذلك من جراك ومن جرائك أي من أجلك (sihah)

## ت ح ت (root_000177): 14:23 تَحْتِهَا

- **B001** alt konum — alt, altinda kalan yer

  اللَّبّة موضع القلادة من الصدر (maqayis;sihah)؛ اللَّبّة باطن العنق (jamhara)؛ اللَّبّة المنحر (sihah;tahdhib)؛ اللبب موضع القلادة من الصدر (maqayis;sihah)؛ لببت الرجل ضربت لبته (maqayis;mufradat)؛ لببت فلانا إذا جمعت ثيابه عند صدره ونحره (sihah;tahdhib)؛ متلببا به أي تحزم بثوبه عند صدره (tahdhib)؛ لبب الفرس (maqayis)؛ ما يشد على صدر الدابة (sihah)
- **B006** kumun incelip ovaya bağlanan yakın ucu [kalıp] — kumun dağa veya kum sırtına yakın, incelen uç bölümü
  اللبب من الرمل ما كان قريبا من جبل متصلا بسهل (maqayis)؛ اللبب ما استرق من الرمل (sihah)؛ اللبب من الرمل ما كان قريبا من حبل الرمل (tahdhib)
- **B007** ağaca sarılan, sağaltımda kullanılan ot — ağaca sarılarak büyüyen ve sağaltımda kullanılan ot
  اللبلاب نبت (maqayis)؛ اللبلاب نبت يلتوي على الشجر (sihah)؛ اللبلاب بقلة معروفة يتداوى بها (tahdhib)
- **B008** ince duygunun kaynağı sayılan kalp damarları — ince duygunun kaynağı sayılan kalp damarları
  بنات ألبب عروق في القلب يكون منها الرقة (sihah)
- **B009** yarık üstlüğe benzetilen giysi — yarık üstlüğe benzetilen bir giysi
  اللبيبة ثوب كالبقيرة (sihah)
- **B010** bolluk, esenlik ve güven içinde olma [kalıp] — bolluk, genişlik ve güven içinde olmak
  فلان في لبب رخي إذا كان في حال واسعة (sihah)؛ في سعة وخصب وأمن (tahdhib)؛ في سعة (mufradat)
- **B011** koyun sürüsünün toplu gürültüsü — koyun sürüsünün toplu sesleri ve gürültüsü
  لبالب الغنم جلبتها وأصواتها (sihah)

(source: latent_activation/network/v3/reviews/s014/reader_a_pilot.md)

# s014 Semantic Channel Discovery

## Parent Channels

### 1. P1 (14:1-15): Message Made Visible and Traversable
- Semantic invariant: Guidance converts what is concealed into something publicly perceptible, linguistically transmissible, and spatially followable.
- Surface relation: direct; 14:1-5, 9-12: كِتَابٌ، أَنزَلْنَاهُ، لِتُخْرِجَ، الظُّلُمَاتِ، النُّورِ، صِرَاطِ، سَبِيلِ، بِلِسَانِ، لِيُبَيِّنَ، أَرْسَلْنَا، رُسُلُهُمْ، أُرْسِلْتُمْ، تَدْعُونَنَا، سُلْطَانٍ مُبِينٍ، هَدَانَا سُبُلَنَا.
- Surprising reach: Lexical branches materialize revelation as extraction from cover, road engineering, a route to water, a public thoroughfare, oral articulation, and a quietly imparted report.

#### Subchannel A. Extraction from Cover into Light
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A written and delivered message draws people out of concealment and makes its content appear under illumination.
- Active motifs: written letters/book `quranic:root_001283:B002/m01`; lowering as delivery `quranic:root_001492:B002/m01`; extraction from concealment `quranic:root_000400:B002/m01`; disappearance under cover `quranic:root_000913:B002/m01`; blocking or concealing truth `quranic:root_001307:B003/m01`; illumination `quranic:root_001564:B001/m01`; appearance and disclosure `quranic:root_000170:B004/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:1-5: كِتَابٌ، أَنزَلْنَاهُ، لِتُخْرِجَ، الظُّلُمَاتِ، النُّورِ، الْكَافِرِينَ، ضَلَالٍ، لِيُبَيِّنَ، أَخْرِجْ.
- Synthesis: The message is not only information. Its lexical scene is a transfer that reaches a covered location, removes its occupants, and places both people and meaning in light. Concealment, denial, and straying are therefore spatial resistance to disclosure.

#### Subchannel B. Route Orientation and Obstruction
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A traveler is aligned to an extended, clear road while obstruction, bending, and wandering deform access to it.
- Active motifs: straight road `quranic:root_000858:B001/m01`; extended path `quranic:root_000672:B001/m01`; road leading to water `quranic:root_000848:B004/m01`; physical bending `quranic:root_001057:B001/m01`; semantic deviation `quranic:root_001057:B002/m01`; wandering from the way `quranic:root_000913:B001/m01`; gentle direction to a path `quranic:root_001583:B001/m01`; clear road across easy ground `quranic:root_001464:B007/m01`; guiding beacon `quranic:root_001564:B005/m01`; public thoroughfare and alignment `quranic:root_000009:B010/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:1-3, 9, 12: النُّورِ، صِرَاطِ، يَصُدُّونَ عَنْ سَبِيلِ، يَبْغُونَهَا عِوَجًا، ضَلَالٍ، يَأْتِكُمْ، نَبَؤُا، هَدَانَا سُبُلَنَا.
- Synthesis: Guidance is staged as practical orientation: a straight, extended, conspicuous route with a beacon and even access to water. The opposing operations are not one generic error but blockage, imposed curvature, and loss of bearing.

#### Subchannel C. Embodied and Local Transmission
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A dispatched messenger carries a report into the hearers' own speech, articulates it bodily, and makes its force locally intelligible.
- Active motifs: dispatching `quranic:root_000563:B001/m01`; messenger and carried message `quranic:root_000563:B002/m01`; language and speech `quranic:root_001355:B006/m01`; linguistic delivery of a message `quranic:root_001355:B008/m01`; disclosure by speech or sign `quranic:root_000170:B005/m01`; mouth opening in speech `quranic:root_001190:B003/m01`; uttered speech `quranic:root_001272:B001/m01`; quiet imparting `quranic:root_001633:B001/m01`; divinely imparted report `quranic:root_001633:B004/m01`; receptive hearing `quranic:root_000022:B002/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:4, 9, 11, 13: أَرْسَلْنَا، رَسُولٍ، بِلِسَانِ، لِيُبَيِّنَ، رُسُلُهُمْ، أَفْوَاهِهِمْ، قَالُوا، أُرْسِلْتُمْ، بِإِذْنِ، لِرُسُلِهِمْ، فَأَوْحَى.
- Synthesis: Dispatch, language, mouth, utterance, and receptive hearing form one communication circuit. Revelation reaches a community by being embodied in its speech, while quiet imparting to the messengers feeds the public act of clarification.

### 2. P1 (14:1-15): Coercive Extraction Reversed into Settlement
- Semantic invariant: Human power expels, strips, and threatens; divine deliverance separates people from that pressure and reallocates land and outcome.
- Surface relation: direct; 14:6, 13-15: أَنجَاكُم، يَسُومُونَكُمْ، يُذَبِّحُونَ، يَسْتَحْيُونَ، بَلَاءٌ، لَنُخْرِجَنَّكُمْ، أَرْضِنَا، لَتَعُودُنَّ، لَنُهْلِكَنَّ، لَنُسْكِنَنَّكُمُ الْأَرْضَ، وَعِيدِ، اسْتَفْتَحُوا، خَابَ، جَبَّارٍ عَنِيدٍ.
- Surprising reach: The interval turns political persecution into a set of physical operations: slaughter, preservation, stripping, forced movement, resettlement, and finally the opening of a judicial passage.

#### Subchannel A. Coercive Butchery and Selective Preservation
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A dominant household imposes injury by slaughtering one line, preserving another, and using the asymmetry as a trial.
- Active motifs: imposing an evil burden `quranic:root_000764:B002/m01`; painful punishment `quranic:root_000994:B005/m01`; slitting the throat `quranic:root_000508:B001/m01`; offspring and lineage `quranic:root_000156:B007/m01`; women `quranic:root_001500:B001/m01`; preserving life by leaving un-killed `quranic:root_000383:B006/m01`; deliverance by separation `quranic:root_001476:B001/m01`; stripping or extraction `quranic:root_001476:B003/m01`; trial that exposes condition `quranic:root_000153:B002/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:6-7: أَنجَاكُم، يَسُومُونَكُمْ، سُوءَ الْعَذَابِ، يُذَبِّحُونَ، أَبْنَاءَكُمْ، يَسْتَحْيُونَ، نِسَاءَكُمْ، بَلَاءٌ، عَذَابِي.
- Synthesis: The scene separates coercion into precise acts rather than a general oppression: injury is imposed, throats are cut, descendants are selected, and some lives are deliberately retained. Deliverance reverses the same mechanics by separating and extracting the exposed community.

#### Subchannel B. Forced Return versus Reallotted Dwelling
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Threatened expulsion and coerced return are answered by the removal of oppressors and the settlement of the displaced in land.
- Active motifs: coercion `quranic:root_000216:B003/m01`; exit and removal `quranic:root_000400:B001/m01`; land and estrangement `quranic:root_000025:B004/m01`; return after departure `quranic:root_001058:B001/m01`; inhabiting and settling `quranic:root_000726:B002/m01`; ruin `quranic:root_001596:B001/m01`; expected harm and fear `quranic:root_000447:B001/m01`; articulated threat `quranic:root_001662:B002/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:13-15: لَنُخْرِجَنَّكُمْ، أَرْضِنَا، لَتَعُودُنَّ، لَنُهْلِكَنَّ، لَنُسْكِنَنَّكُمُ الْأَرْضَ، خَافَ، وَعِيدِ، جَبَّارٍ.
- Synthesis: Both parties contest the same movement and place. The coercive regime claims authority to eject or force return; the answer removes that regime and turns the threatened outsiders into settled inhabitants.

#### Subchannel C. Opening as Verdict
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Opposed parties seek an opening; the opening becomes judicial decision and victory, while the tyrant's intended outcome fails.
- Active motifs: opening as judicial decision `quranic:root_001124:B003/m01`; opening as victory `quranic:root_001124:B004/m01`; failed aim and deprivation `quranic:root_000451:B001/m01`; ruin `quranic:root_001596:B001/m01`; obstinate deviation `quranic:root_001052:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:13, 15: لَنُهْلِكَنَّ، اسْتَفْتَحُوا، خَابَ، جَبَّارٍ، عَنِيدٍ.
- Synthesis: The requested “opening” is activated as both access and adjudication. What opens for the petitioners closes the tyrant's project: obstinate force reaches deprivation rather than its intended victory.

### 3. P1 (14:1-15): Contested Delegation and Entrusted Endurance
- Semantic invariant: Messenger authority is delegated rather than possessed, so refusal cannot be answered by autonomous force but by evidence, permission, trust, and endurance.
- Surface relation: direct; 14:9-12: بِالْبَيِّنَاتِ، فَرَدُّوا أَيْدِيَهُمْ فِي أَفْوَاهِهِمْ، شَكٍّ مُرِيبٍ، بَشَرٌ مِثْلُنَا، بِسُلْطَانٍ مُبِينٍ، بِإِذْنِ اللَّهِ، فَلْيَتَوَكَّلِ، هَدَانَا سُبُلَنَا، لَنَصْبِرَنَّ، آذَيْتُمُونَا.
- Surprising reach: Refusal becomes bodily recoil and invasive doubt, while reliance becomes the transfer of a matter to a sufficient guardian rather than passive optimism.

#### Subchannel A. Evidence Meets Bodily Refusal
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Clear evidence arrives, but hearers reject it bodily and experience doubt as something penetrating and disturbing their interior order.
- Active motifs: clarity and disclosure `quranic:root_000170:B004/m01`; rejection and non-acceptance `quranic:root_000555:B003/m01`; hand as operative limb `quranic:root_001693:B001/m01`; mouth as bodily aperture `quranic:root_001190:B001/m01`; doubt as interpenetration `quranic:root_000812:B001/m01`; piercing or organizing insertion `quranic:root_000812:B002/m01`; disturbed doubt `quranic:root_000616:B001/m01`; compelling proof `quranic:root_000732:B002/m01`; visible human skin `quranic:root_000120:B002/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:9-10: بِالْبَيِّنَاتِ، فَرَدُّوا، أَيْدِيَهُمْ، أَفْوَاهِهِمْ، كَفَرْنَا، شَكٍّ، مُرِيبٍ، بَشَرٌ، بِسُلْطَانٍ مُبِينٍ.
- Synthesis: The response to proof is rendered as an embodied counter-motion: hands and mouths participate in refusal, while doubt enters and disorders. The bodily image usefully pressures a purely propositional reading of disbelief.

#### Subchannel B. Authority Held by Permission
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Human messengers carry proof and report, but effective authority remains contingent on permission from the commissioning lord.
- Active motifs: messenger and message `quranic:root_000563:B002/m01`; visible human embodiment `quranic:root_000120:B002/m01`; compelling proof `quranic:root_000732:B002/m01`; power and authority `quranic:root_000732:B003/m01`; authorization `quranic:root_000022:B004/m01`; lordship and nurture `quranic:root_000532:B001/m01`; divinely imparted report `quranic:root_001633:B004/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:10-11, 13: رُسُلُهُمْ، بَشَرٌ، بِسُلْطَانٍ مُبِينٍ، بِإِذْنِ اللَّهِ، رَبُّهُمْ، فَأَوْحَى.
- Synthesis: Proof, authority, permission, and revelation form a delegated chain. The messenger's ordinary embodiment does not cancel the commission; it locates power outside the human carrier and prevents the sign from becoming personal possession.

#### Subchannel C. Reliance as Entrusting under Injury
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The messengers hand the threatened matter to a sufficient guardian, restrain panic, and continue along their assigned paths under injury.
- Active motifs: handing a matter over `quranic:root_001681:B001/m01`; reliance `quranic:root_001681:B002/m01`; guardian sufficiency `quranic:root_001681:B006/m01`; heart-security and trust `quranic:root_000054:B001/m01`; restraining the self from panic `quranic:root_000840:B001/m01`; distress with no visible exit `quranic:root_000840:B006/m01`; harmful injury or speech `quranic:root_000023:B001/m01`; guidance to a path `quranic:root_001583:B001/m01`; anticipated harm `quranic:root_000447:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:11-14: فَلْيَتَوَكَّلِ الْمُؤْمِنُونَ، نَتَوَكَّلَ، هَدَانَا سُبُلَنَا، لَنَصْبِرَنَّ، آذَيْتُمُونَا، الْمُتَوَكِّلُونَ، خَافَ.
- Synthesis: Reliance is a transfer of custody under real pressure. Endurance then appears as controlled continuation through injury and apparent enclosure, not denial that harm or fear exists.

### 4. P2 (14:16-27): Blocked Intake and Suspended Death
- Semantic invariant: What should sustain life enters as hostile matter, fails to pass through the body, and holds the sufferer at the threshold of death without release.
- Surface relation: direct; 14:16-17: يُسْقَى، مَاءٍ صَدِيدٍ، يَتَجَرَّعُهُ، لَا يَكَادُ يُسِيغُهُ، يَأْتِيهِ الْمَوْتُ، وَمَا هُوَ بِمَيِّتٍ، عَذَابٌ غَلِيظٌ.
- Surprising reach: Watering, irrigation share, dosage, throat mechanics, breath, interior disease, stillness, and death-as-rest build a physiological scene of denied nourishment and denied termination.

#### Subchannel A. Tainted Watering
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A recipient is watered, but the administered liquid is a hated, diseased substance rather than a life-giving share.
- Active motifs: quenching thirst `quranic:root_000722:B001/m01`; providing drink `quranic:root_000722:B002/m01`; irrigation share or channel `quranic:root_000722:B003/m01`; drenching the heart with a hated thing `quranic:root_000722:B010/m01`; water `quranic:root_001458:B001/m01`; pouring or delivering water `quranic:root_001458:B003/m01`; festering abscess and pus `quranic:root_000025:B011/m01`; body refusing food or drink `quranic:root_000994:B002/m01`; disease consuming the interior `quranic:root_001642:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:16-17, 19, 26: مِنْ وَرَائِهِ، يُسْقَى، مَاءٍ، صَدِيدٍ، عَذَابٌ، الْأَرْضَ، الْأَرْضِ.
- Synthesis: The scene reverses irrigation. A measured share is delivered, yet its material is suppurating and internally destructive; the same act that ordinarily restores life becomes forced intake of corruption.

#### Subchannel B. Throat Passage under Failure
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: The sufferer takes repeated measured gulps, but the liquid cannot achieve smooth passage through the throat.
- Active motifs: swallowed gulp `quranic:root_000237:B001/m01`; chin or last-breath posture `quranic:root_000237:B004/m01`; smooth throat passage `quranic:root_000761:B001/m01`; laborious attempt `quranic:root_001329:B001/m01`; nearing an action `quranic:root_001329:B002/m01`; inability under negation `quranic:root_001329:B003/m01`; administered sips `quranic:root_001188:B007/m01`; oppressive thickness `quranic:root_001099:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:17, 26: يَتَجَرَّعُهُ، لَا يَكَادُ، يُسِيغُهُ، عَذَابٌ غَلِيظٌ، فَوْقِ.
- Synthesis: Gulp, dosage, near-performance, failed swallowing, and thickness isolate the operation at issue. Punishment is materialized as perpetual almost-ingestion: the body repeatedly approaches completion but cannot complete it.

#### Subchannel C. Death Arrives but Does Not Discharge
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Death approaches from every side and deadens the body, yet the terminal decree and its release remain withheld.
- Active motifs: arrival `quranic:root_000009:B001/m01`; loss of life and strength `quranic:root_001454:B001/m01`; deadening of the heart `quranic:root_001454:B006/m01`; fainting or swoon `quranic:root_001454:B009/m01`; bodily stillness `quranic:root_001454:B012/m01`; death as rest or release `quranic:root_000609:B016/m01`; death decree `quranic:root_001237:B003/m01`; the side behind or beyond `quranic:root_001642:B006/m01`; breath issuing from the interior `quranic:root_001533:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:17-18, 22: يَأْتِيهِ، الْمَوْتُ، كُلِّ مَكَانٍ، مَيِّتٍ، مِنْ وَرَائِهِ، الرِّيحُ، قُضِيَ، أَنْفُسَكُمْ.
- Synthesis: Arrival, breath, deadening, swoon, and stillness accumulate signs of dying, but death's decisive and relieving function does not occur. The result is a body held in a recurrent terminal state.

### 5. P2 (14:16-27): Supports Exposed and Withdrawn
- Semantic invariant: Works, created continuities, and social dependencies appear stable until the force supporting them is withdrawn and their inability to bear weight is exposed.
- Surface relation: direct; 14:18-22: أَعْمَالُهُمْ كَرَمَادٍ، اشْتَدَّتْ بِهِ الرِّيحُ، يَوْمٍ عَاصِفٍ، لَا يَقْدِرُونَ، كَسَبُوا، يُذْهِبْكُمْ، خَلْقٍ جَدِيدٍ، بَرَزُوا، الضُّعَفَاءُ، اسْتَكْبَرُوا، تَبَعًا، مُغْنُونَ، مَحِيصٍ، وَعَدَكُمْ، أَخْلَفْتُكُمْ، سُلْطَانٍ، دَعَوْتُكُمْ، اسْتَجَبْتُمْ، مُصْرِخِكُمْ.
- Surprising reach: Chaff, gale, exposed ground, liability, broken promises, and failed rescue translate moral loss into structural failure across material, created, and political supports.

#### Subchannel A. Work Dispersed as Ash and Chaff
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Accumulated work is reduced to light residue and carried away by a violent wind, leaving no recoverable gain.
- Active motifs: intentional work `quranic:root_001046:B001/m01`; ash `quranic:root_000598:B002/m01`; wind `quranic:root_000609:B003/m01`; wind as force `quranic:root_000609:B011/m01`; chaff or husk `quranic:root_001020:B001/m01`; gale `quranic:root_001020:B002/m01`; sweeping destruction `quranic:root_001020:B004/m01`; strength `quranic:root_000782:B002/m01`; capacity `quranic:root_001205:B003/m01`; acquired gain `quranic:root_001296:B001/m01`; loss `quranic:root_000913:B003/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:18, 20: أَعْمَالُهُمْ، رَمَادٍ، اشْتَدَّتْ، الرِّيحُ، عَاصِفٍ، لَا يَقْدِرُونَ، كَسَبُوا، بِعَزِيزٍ.
- Synthesis: The image distinguishes production from durability. Work and gain can exist, yet when reduced to ash or husk they have no cohesion against the gale; power is registered precisely as the inability to retain what was acquired.

#### Subchannel B. Creation Removed and Replaced
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A formed creation can be dismissed, and a newly cut beginning can be brought forward in its place.
- Active motifs: measuring `quranic:root_000434:B001/m01`; creating `quranic:root_000434:B002/m01`; completing a form `quranic:root_000434:B003/m01`; departure and removal `quranic:root_000522:B006/m01`; bringing into arrival `quranic:root_000009:B001/m01`; newness after a cut `quranic:root_000227:B004/m01`; exposure `quranic:root_000105:B001/m01`; gathering `quranic:root_000259:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:19, 21: خَلَقَ، يُذْهِبْكُمْ، يَأْتِ، بِخَلْقٍ جَدِيدٍ، بَرَزُوا، جَمِيعًا.
- Synthesis: Creation is pictured as measured formation rather than an untouchable possession. Removal cuts one continuity; arrival and new formation establish another, anticipating the exposed gathering that follows.

#### Subchannel C. Hierarchy without Load-Bearing Power
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: Followers seek protection from their former superiors, but rank cannot carry liability, avert punishment, or open an escape.
- Active motifs: exposure `quranic:root_000105:B001/m01`; gathering `quranic:root_000259:B001/m01`; social joining `quranic:root_000259:B002/m01`; weakness `quranic:root_000909:B001/m01`; arrogance `quranic:root_001281:B006/m01`; following `quranic:root_000175:B001/m01`; liability attached to following `quranic:root_000175:B006/m01`; insufficient protection `quranic:root_001110:B002/m01`; turning away or escape `quranic:root_000378:B001/m01`; equivalence of alternatives `quranic:root_000766:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:21: بَرَزُوا، جَمِيعًا، الضُّعَفَاءُ، اسْتَكْبَرُوا، تَبَعًا، مُغْنُونَ، سَوَاءٌ، مَحِيصٍ.
- Synthesis: Gathering exposes the social frame as a failed support system. Following created liability but no reciprocal capacity; once rank is tested by punishment, panic and endurance become equal because neither opens an exit.

#### Subchannel D. Promise, Call, and Failed Rescue
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A claimant opens hope through a promise and summons, breaks the promise after compliance, and disclaims both authority and rescue.
- Active motifs: promise opening hope `quranic:root_001662:B001/m01`; broken promise `quranic:root_000433:B005/m01`; authority `quranic:root_000732:B003/m01`; summons `quranic:root_000478:B001/m01`; answering a call `quranic:root_000273:B003/m01`; blame `quranic:root_001386:B001/m01`; cry for rescue `quranic:root_000855:B002/m01`; rescuing response `quranic:root_000855:B003/m01`; repudiation `quranic:root_001307:B005/m01`; partnership `quranic:root_000791:B001/m01`; associating a partner `quranic:root_000791:B002/m01`; decisive decree `quranic:root_001237:B001/m01`; completed determination `quranic:root_001237:B004/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:22: قُضِيَ الْأَمْرُ، وَعَدَكُمْ، وَعْدَ، وَعَدْتُكُمْ، أَخْلَفْتُكُمْ، سُلْطَانٍ، دَعَوْتُكُمْ، اسْتَجَبْتُمْ، تَلُومُونِي، مُصْرِخِكُمْ، أَشْرَكْتُمُونِ، كَفَرْتُ.
- Synthesis: The speech reconstructs deception as a failed contract and rescue relation. The call secures compliance without coercive authority; once the decree is complete, promise, partnership, and mutual rescue are explicitly severed.

### 6. P2 (14:16-27): Rooted Speech as Living Provision
- Semantic invariant: Beneficial speech and faithful life share an architecture of rooted stability, upward extension, timed yield, and enduring habitation.
- Surface relation: direct; 14:23-27: جَنَّاتٍ تَجْرِي، الْأَنْهَارُ، خَالِدِينَ، تَحِيَّتُهُمْ، سَلَامٌ، كَلِمَةً طَيِّبَةً، شَجَرَةٍ طَيِّبَةٍ، أَصْلُهَا ثَابِتٌ، فَرْعُهَا فِي السَّمَاءِ، تُؤْتِي أُكُلَهَا كُلَّ حِينٍ، كَلِمَةٍ خَبِيثَةٍ، اجْتُثَّتْ، مَا لَهَا مِنْ قَرَارٍ، يُثَبِّتُ، بِالْقَوْلِ الثَّابِتِ.
- Surprising reach: The tree simile extends into hydrology, seasonal allotment, greeting as life-giving safety, and the reinforcement of heart, mind, and tongue.

#### Subchannel A. Garden Flow and Peaceful Permanence
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: Entrants inhabit a concealed garden whose water and provision continue beneath a stable greeting of safety.
- Active motifs: entering `quranic:root_000464:B001/m01`; garden hidden by trees `quranic:root_000266:B003/m01`; flowing `quranic:root_000240:B001/m01`; continuous provision `quranic:root_000240:B006/m01`; river channel `quranic:root_001559:B001/m01`; permanence `quranic:root_000429:B001/m01`; authorization `quranic:root_000022:B004/m01`; safety `quranic:root_000737:B001/m01`; peace `quranic:root_000737:B004/m01`; life-giving greeting `quranic:root_000383:B007/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:23: أُدْخِلَ، جَنَّاتٍ، تَجْرِي، الْأَنْهَارُ، خَالِدِينَ، بِإِذْنِ، تَحِيَّتُهُمْ، سَلَامٌ.
- Synthesis: Entry, enclosure by growth, flowing channels, continuity, and greeting form one habitable ecology. Peace is not detached speech but the social expression of a place whose life-support continues.

#### Subchannel B. Word-Tree Architecture
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: An intelligible good utterance is rendered as a tree whose root fixes below and whose branch extends upward.
- Active motifs: rendering a parable `quranic:root_000906:B003/m01`; parable as likeness `quranic:root_001397:B003/m01`; intelligible speech `quranic:root_001316:B001/m01`; goodness and purity `quranic:root_000961:B001/m01`; tree `quranic:root_000777:B001/m01`; base or root `quranic:root_000038:B001/m01`; rootedness `quranic:root_000038:B002/m01`; permanence `quranic:root_000192:B001/m01`; branch `quranic:root_001146:B001/m01`; sky above `quranic:root_000745:B004/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:24-25: ضَرَبَ، مَثَلًا، كَلِمَةً، طَيِّبَةً، شَجَرَةٍ، أَصْلُهَا، ثَابِتٌ، فَرْعُهَا، السَّمَاءِ، الْأَمْثَالَ.
- Synthesis: Speech becomes an organism with differentiated parts and directions. Intelligibility and purity name its quality; root, stable base, and elevated branch explain how it persists while reaching beyond its point of origin.

#### Subchannel C. Timed Fruit as Allocated Share
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The rooted organism repeatedly produces an edible share at its appointed season under authorization.
- Active motifs: producing or bringing forth `quranic:root_000043:B002/m01`; edible share `quranic:root_000043:B003/m01`; season `quranic:root_000382:B001/m01`; growth and yield `quranic:root_000009:B007/m01`; authorization `quranic:root_000022:B004/m01`; distributed totality `quranic:root_001315:B003/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:25: تُؤْتِي، أُكُلَهَا، كُلَّ، حِينٍ، بِإِذْنِ.
- Synthesis: Stability is productive rather than static. A timed cycle converts rooted life into a recurring allotted share, so the good word's value lies in dependable nourishment as well as enduring form.

#### Subchannel D. Uprooted Speech without Resting Place
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A corrupt word is compared to a corrupt tree torn out from its origin and left without a stable bottom.
- Active motifs: corruption and badness `quranic:root_000386:B001/m01`; tree `quranic:root_000777:B001/m01`; total uprooting from origin `quranic:root_000221:B001/m01`; position above `quranic:root_001188:B001/m01`; ground `quranic:root_000025:B001/m01`; stability and resting place `quranic:root_001215:B003/m01`; stable bottom `quranic:root_001215:B006/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:26: كَلِمَةٍ خَبِيثَةٍ، شَجَرَةٍ خَبِيثَةٍ، اجْتُثَّتْ، فَوْقِ الْأَرْضِ، قَرَارٍ.
- Synthesis: The contrast isolates a different operation and outcome from ordinary fruiting. The whole organism is severed at origin, placed only above the ground, and denied the lower support required for continuity.

#### Subchannel E. Human Fixing through Stable Speech
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Stable speech reinforces the believer's interior and verbal posture across present and later life.
- Active motifs: fixing heart, mind, or tongue `quranic:root_000192:B002/m01`; reinforcing `quranic:root_000192:B003/m01`; faith `quranic:root_000054:B002/m01`; uttered speech `quranic:root_001272:B001/m01`; life and benefit `quranic:root_000383:B013/m01`; lower or present world `quranic:root_000493:B002/m01`; later or other life `quranic:root_000019:B001/m01`; misdirection `quranic:root_000913:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:27: يُثَبِّتُ، آمَنُوا، بِالْقَوْلِ الثَّابِتِ، الْحَيَاةِ الدُّنْيَا، الْآخِرَةِ، يُضِلُّ.
- Synthesis: The tree's stable base is transferred to human cognition and speech. Fixing is an active reinforcement that carries the believer through two temporal settings, while misdirection names the loss of that orientation.

### 7. P3 (14:28-41): Misplaced Exchange and Ruined Dwelling
- Semantic invariant: A beneficial allotment is deliberately exchanged for its contrary, and the altered valuation materializes as a damaged destination for a whole people.
- Surface relation: direct; 14:28-30: بَدَّلُوا نِعْمَتَ اللَّهِ كُفْرًا، أَحَلُّوا قَوْمَهُمْ دَارَ الْبَوَارِ، جَهَنَّمَ يَصْلَوْنَهَا، بِئْسَ الْقَرَارُ، أَنْدَادًا، لِيُضِلُّوا عَنْ سَبِيلِهِ، تَمَتَّعُوا، مَصِيرَكُمْ إِلَى النَّارِ.
- Surprising reach: Exchange becomes land degradation: substitution does not merely change belief but settles a population in stagnation, barrenness, decay, and fire.

#### Subchannel A. Blessing Substituted into Ruin
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: An allotted good is replaced by concealment, and the replacement settles a people in a barren, decaying abode.
- Active motifs: substitution `quranic:root_000095:B001/m01`; blessing and good condition `quranic:root_001525:B001/m01`; covering a blessing `quranic:root_001307:B004/m01`; causing to lodge `quranic:root_000351:B002/m01`; people as a gathered community `quranic:root_001273:B001/m01`; ruin `quranic:root_000164:B001/m01`; stagnation into decay `quranic:root_000164:B002/m01`; barren or emptied land `quranic:root_000164:B003/m01`; fixed destination `quranic:root_001215:B003/m01`; entering fire `quranic:root_000880:B003/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:28-29, 34: بَدَّلُوا، نِعْمَتَ، كُفْرًا، أَحَلُّوا، قَوْمَهُمْ، دَارَ الْبَوَارِ، يَصْلَوْنَهَا، الْقَرَارُ، نِعْمَتَ، كَفَّارٌ.
- Synthesis: Moral substitution produces a habitat. The branch imagery unfolds ruin as stopped circulation, decay, and barrenness, making the destination the material form of what happened to the original blessing.

#### Subchannel B. Rival Routes to Temporary Use
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Contrary counterparts divert travelers from the route, offer a bounded interval of use, and terminate in fire.
- Active motifs: making into a state `quranic:root_000248:B001/m01`; contrary counterpart or rival `quranic:root_001484:B002/m01`; misdirection `quranic:root_000913:B001/m01`; extended path `quranic:root_000672:B001/m01`; enjoyed benefit `quranic:root_001395:B001/m01`; extended but temporary use `quranic:root_001395:B007/m01`; becoming and outcome `quranic:root_000897:B001/m01`; destination `quranic:root_000897:B007/m01`; fire `quranic:root_001564:B002/m01`; entering fire `quranic:root_000880:B003/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:29-30: يَصْلَوْنَهَا، جَعَلُوا، أَنْدَادًا، لِيُضِلُّوا، سَبِيلِهِ، تَمَتَّعُوا، مَصِيرَكُمْ، النَّارِ.
- Synthesis: The rivals operate as route-altering counterparts. Their benefit is real enough to be used but temporally bounded; the path's final conversion into fire discloses the cost hidden by present enjoyment.

### 8. P3 (14:28-41): Provision as Circulating Infrastructure
- Semantic invariant: Provision reaches life through ordered outward and inward flows: spending, rainfall, fruiting, transport, river courses, celestial cycles, and gifts.
- Surface relation: direct; 14:31-34: يُنْفِقُوا مِمَّا رَزَقْنَاهُمْ، سِرًّا وَعَلَانِيَةً، لَا بَيْعٌ فِيهِ وَلَا خِلَالٌ، أَنْزَلَ مِنَ السَّمَاءِ مَاءً، فَأَخْرَجَ بِهِ مِنَ الثَّمَرَاتِ رِزْقًا، سَخَّرَ لَكُمُ الْفُلْكَ، تَجْرِي فِي الْبَحْرِ، الْأَنْهَارَ، الشَّمْسَ وَالْقَمَرَ دَائِبَيْنِ، اللَّيْلَ وَالنَّهَارَ، آتَاكُمْ، تَعُدُّوا، لَا تُحْصُوهَا.
- Surprising reach: The interval links ritual expenditure to tunnels and outlets, rainfall to delivery, boats to circular motion, dawn to opening, and blessing to an unfinishable account.

#### Subchannel A. Expenditure before Exchange Closes
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Allocated provision exits through visible and concealed channels before a day when sale and social exchange no longer operate.
- Active motifs: owned servant `quranic:root_000973:B001/m01`; faith `quranic:root_000054:B002/m01`; bodily standing `quranic:root_001273:B002/m01`; rising to perform a task `quranic:root_001273:B003/m01`; ritual prayer `quranic:root_000879:B003/m01`; wealth exiting in expenditure `quranic:root_001537:B002/m01`; tunnel or outlet `quranic:root_001537:B003/m01`; visible entrance and hidden exit `quranic:root_001537:B004/m01`; allotted gift `quranic:root_000560:B001/m01`; concealment `quranic:root_000697:B001/m01`; disclosure `quranic:root_001041:B001/m01`; sale or exchange `quranic:root_000169:B001/m01`; gap or separation `quranic:root_000435:B001/m01`; friendship `quranic:root_000435:B003/m01`; prior time `quranic:root_001198:B002/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:31: لِعِبَادِيَ، آمَنُوا، يُقِيمُوا، الصَّلَاةَ، يُنْفِقُوا، رَزَقْنَاهُمْ، سِرًّا، عَلَانِيَةً، مِنْ قَبْلِ، بَيْعٌ، خِلَالٌ.
- Synthesis: The addressed servants direct allocated provision outward. The spending root's outlet and tunnel imagery makes concealed and visible expenditure two apertures of one circulation, set against the later closure of market and friendship.

#### Subchannel B. Sky-Water-Fruit Delivery Chain
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: Water descends from above, is delivered into ground, and brings fruit outward as food provision.
- Active motifs: descent `quranic:root_001492:B001/m01`; lowering as delivery `quranic:root_001492:B002/m01`; sky above `quranic:root_000745:B004/m01`; water `quranic:root_001458:B001/m01`; extraction and emergence `quranic:root_000400:B002/m01`; ripe fruit `quranic:root_000205:B001/m01`; food provision `quranic:root_000560:B002/m01`; rain as provision `quranic:root_000560:B003/m01`; fertile ground `quranic:root_000025:B002/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:32: السَّمَاوَاتِ، الْأَرْضَ، أَنْزَلَ، السَّمَاءِ، مَاءً، فَأَخْرَجَ، الثَّمَرَاتِ، رِزْقًا.
- Synthesis: The verse's sequence is a supply chain: descent is delivery, ground is the receiving medium, emergence is production, and fruit is the allocated output. Provision is therefore an ordered transfer rather than an isolated gift.

#### Subchannel C. Ship, Sea, and River Routing
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Vessels and watercourses are harnessed so that goods and travelers can circulate through broad and bounded channels.
- Active motifs: harnessing and driving toward a purpose `quranic:root_000685:B001/m01`; vessel circulating on water `quranic:root_001177:B003/m01`; circular motion `quranic:root_001177:B001/m01`; flowing `quranic:root_000240:B001/m01`; broad water `quranic:root_000086:B001/m01`; directing command `quranic:root_000051:B002/m01`; river channel `quranic:root_001559:B001/m01`; guiding a watercourse `quranic:root_000009:B004/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:31-32, 34: يَأْتِيَ، سَخَّرَ، الْفُلْكَ، لِتَجْرِيَ، الْبَحْرِ، بِأَمْرِهِ، سَخَّرَ، الْأَنْهَارَ، آتَاكُمْ.
- Synthesis: Sea, vessel, command, flow, and river are differentiated components of controlled circulation. Harnessing does not still the water; it makes motion reliably serve an assigned route.

#### Subchannel D. Celestial Continuous Labor
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Sun, moon, night, and day move in continuous assigned labor, repeatedly opening and closing temporal access.
- Active motifs: harnessing to purpose `quranic:root_000685:B001/m01`; sun `quranic:root_000818:B001/m01`; moon `quranic:root_001255:B001/m01`; continuous labor `quranic:root_000456:B001/m01`; night `quranic:root_001392:B001/m01`; daylight as opening `quranic:root_001559:B002/m01`; day `quranic:root_001700:B001/m01`; celestial circulation `quranic:root_001177:B002/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:31-33: يَوْمٌ، الْفُلْكَ، سَخَّرَ، الشَّمْسَ، الْقَمَرَ، دَائِبَيْنِ، اللَّيْلَ، النَّهَارَ.
- Synthesis: The celestial bodies and alternating times are workers within the same provision system. Their repeated circulation and the opening of daylight make temporal regularity another infrastructure made available to human life.

#### Subchannel E. Gifts beyond Exhaustive Enumeration
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Requests meet giving across a total field of needs, but the resulting benefits exceed the recipient's capacity to count or close the account.
- Active motifs: giving `quranic:root_000009:B002/m01`; requesting `quranic:root_000661:B001/m01`; totality `quranic:root_001315:B003/m01`; counting `quranic:root_000989:B001/m01`; exhaustive enumeration `quranic:root_000332:B002/m01`; blessing `quranic:root_001525:B001/m01`; account and calculation `quranic:root_000318:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:34, 41: آتَاكُمْ، كُلِّ، سَأَلْتُمُوهُ، تَعُدُّوا، نِعْمَتَ، لَا تُحْصُوهَا، الْحِسَابُ.
- Synthesis: Giving answers asking, yet the total granted field cannot be reduced to a completed inventory. Counting and exhaustive enumeration are activated only to fail, reframing abundance as an account whose entries continue beyond human closure.

### 9. P3 (14:28-41): Sanctuary Household as Cultivated Continuity
- Semantic invariant: A household is planted in bounded space so worship, safety, provision, responsive hearing, and lineage can continue across generations.
- Surface relation: direct; 14:35-41: الْبَلَدَ آمِنًا، اجْنُبْنِي وَبَنِيَّ، نَعْبُدَ الْأَصْنَامَ، أَسْكَنْتُ مِنْ ذُرِّيَّتِي بِوَادٍ غَيْرِ ذِي زَرْعٍ، عِنْدَ بَيْتِكَ الْمُحَرَّمِ، يُقِيمُوا الصَّلَاةَ، أَفْئِدَةً، تَهْوِي إِلَيْهِمْ، ارْزُقْهُمْ مِنَ الثَّمَرَاتِ، وَهَبَ لِي، سَمِيعُ الدُّعَاءِ، مِنْ ذُرِّيَّتِي، تَقَبَّلْ دُعَاءِ، وَلِوَالِدَيَّ، يَقُومُ الْحِسَابُ.
- Surprising reach: The prayer resembles cultivation: a small dispersed household is fixed in a flood-course without crops, then sustained by inward-moving hearts, imported fruit, heard calls, and intergenerational acceptance.

#### Subchannel A. Bounded Safety around Worship
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A bounded settlement is made secure while lineage is kept at a distance from embodied cult objects.
- Active motifs: bounded land `quranic:root_000148:B001/m01`; residence within bounds `quranic:root_000148:B009/m01`; safety and heart-security `quranic:root_000054:B001/m01`; making into a state `quranic:root_000248:B002/m01`; keeping at a distance `quranic:root_000262:B003/m01`; offspring and lineage `quranic:root_000156:B007/m01`; worship `quranic:root_000973:B003/m01`; embodied idol `quranic:root_000886:B001/m01`; sanctuary boundary `quranic:root_000313:B003/m01`; sanctity and protected right `quranic:root_000313:B006/m01`; the House as sacred center `quranic:root_000156:B003/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:35-37: اجْعَلْ، الْبَلَدَ، آمِنًا، اجْنُبْنِي، بَنِيَّ، نَعْبُدَ، الْأَصْنَامَ، ذُرِّيَّتِي، بَيْتِكَ الْمُحَرَّمِ.
- Synthesis: Security is spatially and ritually bounded. The prayer asks both for a protected center and for distance from rival embodied centers, so household continuity depends on where bodies dwell and toward what they worship.

#### Subchannel B. Settlement in an Uncultivated Watercourse
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Part of a lineage is fixed near the sacred house in a valley capable of channeling water but presently without cultivation.
- Active motifs: settling `quranic:root_000726:B002/m01`; sustenance that fixes residence `quranic:root_000726:B010/m01`; tiny dispersed particle `quranic:root_000511:B001/m01`; valley as flood-course `quranic:root_001637:B005/m01`; cultivation `quranic:root_000630:B001/m01`; sheltering house `quranic:root_000166:B001/m01`; sanctuary boundary `quranic:root_000313:B003/m01`; ritual prayer `quranic:root_000879:B003/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:37: أَسْكَنْتُ، ذُرِّيَّتِي، بِوَادٍ، غَيْرِ ذِي زَرْعٍ، عِنْدَ بَيْتِكَ الْمُحَرَّمِ، لِيُقِيمُوا الصَّلَاةَ.
- Synthesis: Settlement is staged like planting under difficult hydrological conditions. The lineage is a small dispersed beginning placed in a flood-course without crops; house, sanctuary, prayer, and future sustenance are the supports intended to make residence hold.

#### Subchannel C. Hearts and Fruit Drawn Inward
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Human interiors incline toward the settlers while fruit provision is drawn into the barren site, producing gratitude.
- Active motifs: heart as inner heat `quranic:root_001122:B002/m01`; desire and inclination `quranic:root_001609:B004/m01`; allotted provision `quranic:root_000560:B001/m01`; ripe fruit `quranic:root_000205:B001/m01`; recognition of blessing `quranic:root_000810:B001/m01`; people as community `quranic:root_000059:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:37: أَفْئِدَةً، مِنَ النَّاسِ، تَهْوِي إِلَيْهِمْ، ارْزُقْهُمْ، الثَّمَرَاتِ، يَشْكُرُونَ.
- Synthesis: Two inward motions answer the valley's lack: people incline affectively and fruit arrives materially. Social attachment and nourishment jointly make the settlement viable, with gratitude as their intended yield.

#### Subchannel D. Heard Gift across Old Age
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A call is heard and answered by an unreciprocated gift of offspring after bodily aging.
- Active motifs: praise `quranic:root_000355:B001/m01`; gift without return `quranic:root_001685:B001/m01`; old age `quranic:root_001281:B004/m01`; hearing `quranic:root_000741:B001/m01`; hearing as understanding and response `quranic:root_000741:B003/m01`; call or prayer `quranic:root_000478:B001/m01`; lordship and nurture `quranic:root_000532:B001/m01`; offspring `quranic:root_001683:B001/m01`; tiny dispersed particle `quranic:root_000511:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:39-41: الْحَمْدُ، وَهَبَ، عَلَى الْكِبَرِ، إِسْمَاعِيلَ، إِسْحَاقَ، رَبِّي، سَمِيعُ الدُّعَاءِ، دُعَاءِ، ذُرِّيَّتِي، لِوَالِدَيَّ.
- Synthesis: Hearing is activated as responsive uptake rather than bare perception. The gift of descendants after old age answers the call and extends the small planted household beyond the petitioner's bodily horizon.

#### Subchannel E. Intergenerational Prayer toward Account
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Prayer asks that worship stand, calls be accepted, and forgiveness extend through descendants, parents, and believers when the final account stands.
- Active motifs: lordship and nurture `quranic:root_000532:B001/m01`; causing a state `quranic:root_000248:B002/m01`; bodily standing `quranic:root_001273:B002/m01`; rising at resurrection `quranic:root_001273:B013/m01`; ritual prayer `quranic:root_000879:B003/m01`; offspring `quranic:root_000511:B001/m01`; accepting and receiving `quranic:root_001198:B004/m01`; prayerful call `quranic:root_000478:B001/m01`; covering by forgiveness `quranic:root_001096:B002/m01`; parents `quranic:root_001683:B002/m01`; faith `quranic:root_000054:B002/m01`; decisive day `quranic:root_001700:B003/m01`; account `quranic:root_000318:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:40-41: رَبِّ، اجْعَلْنِي، مُقِيمَ الصَّلَاةِ، ذُرِّيَّتِي، تَقَبَّلْ دُعَاءِ، اغْفِرْ لِي، لِوَالِدَيَّ، لِلْمُؤْمِنِينَ، يَوْمَ يَقُومُ الْحِسَابُ.
- Synthesis: The repeated standing root joins prayer maintained in ordinary life to the rising of the account. Acceptance and forgiveness carry the household frame outward to ancestors and the wider believing community, making continuity accountable rather than merely genealogical.

### 10. P4 (14:42-52): Deferred Reckoning and Arrested Attention
- Semantic invariant: Apparent delay is not neglect; when the marked time arrives, judgment arrests the body's ordinary control of gaze, posture, and interior composure.
- Surface relation: direct; 14:42-43: لَا تَحْسَبَنَّ، غَافِلًا، يَعْمَلُ الظَّالِمُونَ، يُؤَخِّرُهُمْ لِيَوْمٍ، تَشْخَصُ فِيهِ الْأَبْصَارُ، مُهْطِعِينَ، مُقْنِعِي رُءُوسِهِمْ، لَا يَرْتَدُّ إِلَيْهِمْ طَرْفُهُمْ، أَفْئِدَتُهُمْ هَوَاءٌ.
- Surprising reach: Inattention also means failure to mark a record; terror then becomes a coordinated failure of eye return, head movement, blinking, heart-strength, and interior fullness.

#### Subchannel A. The Unneglected Ledger and Deferred Date
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Observers miscalculate delay as inattention, but deeds remain marked until a fixed event exposes the unjust.
- Active motifs: supposing and calculating `quranic:root_000318:B002/m01`; account and reckoning `quranic:root_000318:B001/m01`; inattentiveness `quranic:root_001097:B001/m01`; leaving something unrecorded or unmarked `quranic:root_001097:B002/m01`; intentional deeds `quranic:root_001046:B001/m01`; grievance and demand for redress `quranic:root_000967:B003/m01`; deferral `quranic:root_000019:B002/m01`; severe day or event `quranic:root_001700:B003/m01`; fixed staring `quranic:root_000781:B004/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:42: تَحْسَبَنَّ، غَافِلًا، يَعْمَلُ، الظَّالِمُونَ، يُؤَخِّرُهُمْ، لِيَوْمٍ، تَشْخَصُ.
- Synthesis: The lexical relation between heedlessness and an unmarked record sharpens the denial: postponement has not erased the entry. Miscalculation belongs to the observer, while the deferred date preserves the connection between deed and disclosure.

#### Subchannel B. Gaze and Head Locked Forward
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Bodies rush forward with raised heads and extended necks while sight remains fixed and cannot return through an ordinary blink.
- Active motifs: fixed stare `quranic:root_000781:B004/m01`; upward or arrested posture `quranic:root_000781:B002/m01`; vision `quranic:root_000121:B001/m01`; sustained gaze `quranic:root_001594:B001/m01`; extended neck and raised head `quranic:root_001594:B002/m01`; rushing forward `quranic:root_001594:B003/m01`; elevation without lowering `quranic:root_001263:B004/m01`; head or top `quranic:root_000529:B001/m01`; return `quranic:root_000555:B001/m01`; blink or edge of gaze `quranic:root_000931:B002/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:42-43: تَشْخَصُ، الْأَبْصَارُ، مُهْطِعِينَ، مُقْنِعِي رُءُوسِهِمْ، لَا يَرْتَدُّ، طَرْفُهُمْ.
- Synthesis: The scene is kinematically exact: forward rush combines with vertical extension, while the eye is denied its tiny restorative return. Judgment arrests orientation itself, holding the body in compulsory attention.

#### Subchannel C. The Evacuated Interior
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The exterior remains rigidly directed while the heart loses strength and the interior becomes void.
- Active motifs: heart as inner heat `quranic:root_001122:B002/m01`; weakened or absent heart `quranic:root_001122:B004/m01`; void or air `quranic:root_001609:B001/m01`; hollow wound `quranic:root_001609:B007/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:43: أَفْئِدَتُهُمْ، هَوَاءٌ.
- Synthesis: “Air” becomes an emptied interior rather than mere emotion. The contrast with the locked exterior is severe: posture is overdetermined, but the heart-space that would organize response has lost substance and heat.
