### 11. P4 (14:42-52): Irreversible Return against Human Delay
- Semantic invariant: Humans postpone response and presume continuity, but warning, historical disclosure, material resistance, and unbroken promise converge on an irreversible appointed outcome.
- Surface relation: direct; 14:44-47: أَنْذِرِ، يَأْتِيهِمُ الْعَذَابُ، أَخِّرْنَا إِلَى أَجَلٍ قَرِيبٍ، نُجِبْ دَعْوَتَكَ، نَتَّبِعِ الرُّسُلَ، أَقْسَمْتُمْ، مَا لَكُمْ مِنْ زَوَالٍ، سَكَنْتُمْ فِي مَسَاكِنِ، تَبَيَّنَ لَكُمْ، ضَرَبْنَا لَكُمُ الْأَمْثَالَ، مَكَرُوا مَكْرَهُمْ، لِتَزُولَ مِنْهُ الْجِبَالُ، مُخْلِفَ وَعْدِهِ رُسُلَهُ، عَزِيزٌ ذُو انْتِقَامٍ.
- Surprising reach: The plot root opens an irrigation-and-spoilage scene, while mountain roots supply excavation resistance, woven density, and cohesive ground; the comparison tests how far covert human action can alter a resistant order.

#### Subchannel A. Late Plea for Call-and-Response
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: When warning materializes as arriving punishment, the unjust ask for a short delay in which they promise to answer the call and follow the messengers.
- Active motifs: warning `quranic:root_001488:B001/m01`; arrival `quranic:root_000009:B001/m01`; painful punishment `quranic:root_000994:B005/m01`; deferral `quranic:root_000019:B002/m01`; appointed term `quranic:root_000016:B001/m01`; nearness to expiry `quranic:root_001212:B002/m01`; answering a call `quranic:root_000273:B003/m01`; summons or prayer `quranic:root_000478:B001/m01`; following `quranic:root_000175:B001/m01`; messenger and message `quranic:root_000563:B002/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:44: أَنْذِرِ، يَأْتِيهِمُ، الْعَذَابُ، أَخِّرْنَا، أَجَلٍ قَرِيبٍ، نُجِبْ، دَعْوَتَكَ، نَتَّبِعِ، الرُّسُلَ.
- Synthesis: The plea attempts to reopen the communication circuit after its term has closed. Arrival converts warning into present fact; only then do answer and following become desired operations, but their requested time is itself deferred time.

#### Subchannel B. Oath of No Removal
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A prior oath asserts permanent residence and denies any movement from the present condition.
- Active motifs: oath and swearing `quranic:root_001226:B004/m01`; removal or change of place `quranic:root_000655:B001/m01`; motion after stillness `quranic:root_000655:B002/m01`; occurrence and coming-to-be `quranic:root_001332:B001/m01`; prior time `quranic:root_001198:B002/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:44: تَكُونُوا، أَقْسَمْتُمْ، مِنْ قَبْلُ، مَا لَكُمْ مِنْ زَوَالٍ.
- Synthesis: The oath tries to stabilize one historical condition indefinitely. The removal root activates both displacement and motion after rest, so the denial is not only of death but of any transition capable of breaking present possession.

#### Subchannel C. Inhabited Historical Proof
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Later residents occupy the dwellings of self-wronging predecessors and encounter disclosed actions rendered visible through examples.
- Active motifs: inhabiting and settling `quranic:root_000726:B002/m01`; grievance and demand for redress `quranic:root_000967:B003/m01`; self `quranic:root_001533:B012/m01`; clarity and disclosure `quranic:root_000170:B004/m01`; performed action `quranic:root_001167:B001/m01`; rendering a visible example `quranic:root_000906:B003/m01`; parable as instructive sign `quranic:root_001397:B011/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:45: سَكَنْتُمْ، مَسَاكِنِ، ظَلَمُوا، أَنْفُسَهُمْ، تَبَيَّنَ، فَعَلْنَا، ضَرَبْنَا، الْأَمْثَالَ.
- Synthesis: Historical knowledge is architectural and inhabited. The audience does not merely hear of prior action; it lives inside its surviving spatial result, where disclosure and parable make the predecessor's outcome present.

#### Subchannel D. Covert Irrigation against Mountain Resistance
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A covert action behaves like irrigation capable of penetrating and spoiling growth, yet it confronts mountain material defined by resistance to excavation and cohesive density.
- Active motifs: irrigating land or crop `quranic:root_001438:B005/m01`; spoiled fresh fruit `quranic:root_001438:B006/m01`; removal from place `quranic:root_000655:B001/m01`; mountain as elevated solid mass `quranic:root_000217:B001/m01`; solidity that stops excavation `quranic:root_000217:B005/m01`; tightly woven density `quranic:root_000217:B007/m01`; hard cohesive ground `quranic:root_001008:B007/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:46-47: مَكَرُوا، مَكْرَهُمْ، لِتَزُولَ، الْجِبَالُ، عَزِيزٌ.
- Synthesis: The latent assembly usefully pressures the scale comparison. Plotting can enter indirectly like water and spoil fresh growth, but the object to be moved is simultaneously elevated mass, excavation-resistant substrate, and woven density on cohesive ground. The scene contrasts covert penetration with load-bearing cohesion.

#### Subchannel E. Promise That Cannot Be Broken
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A promise to commissioned messengers carries both hope and threat, remains unbroken, and culminates in overpowering retribution.
- Active motifs: calculating or supposing `quranic:root_000318:B002/m01`; breaking a promise `quranic:root_000433:B005/m01`; promise opening hope `quranic:root_001662:B001/m01`; articulated threat `quranic:root_001662:B002/m01`; messenger and message `quranic:root_000563:B002/m01`; overwhelming might `quranic:root_001008:B002/m01`; retribution `quranic:root_001545:B002/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:47: تَحْسَبَنَّ، مُخْلِفَ، وَعْدِهِ، رُسُلَهُ، عَزِيزٌ، انْتِقَامٍ.
- Synthesis: The same promise structure that failed in 14:22 is here negated as a possibility of failure. Promise, threat, delegated message, might, and retribution form one enforceable commitment rather than an unsupported assurance.

### 12. P4 (14:42-52): Reconstituted Stage and Fitted Punishment
- Semantic invariant: Judgment remakes the shared environment, exposes every participant, fits bodies into constrained relations and materials, and settles each acquisition by exact account.
- Surface relation: direct; 14:48-51: تُبَدَّلُ الْأَرْضُ غَيْرَ الْأَرْضِ وَالسَّمَاوَاتُ، بَرَزُوا، الْوَاحِدِ الْقَهَّارِ، الْمُجْرِمِينَ، مُقَرَّنِينَ فِي الْأَصْفَادِ، سَرَابِيلُهُمْ مِنْ قَطِرَانٍ، تَغْشَى وُجُوهَهُمُ النَّارُ، لِيَجْزِيَ كُلَّ نَفْسٍ مَا كَسَبَتْ، سَرِيعُ الْحِسَابِ.
- Surprising reach: Substitution becomes environmental re-forming; criminal association becomes paired binding; tar is a flowing fitted material; and recompense resembles repayment and debt settlement.

#### Subchannel A. Transformed Ground and Total Exposure
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Ground and sky change form, all occupants emerge onto open terrain, and singular domination becomes unavoidable.
- Active motifs: substitution `quranic:root_000095:B001/m01`; alteration of form `quranic:root_000095:B002/m01`; ground `quranic:root_000025:B001/m01`; transforming or altering `quranic:root_001119:B003/m01`; sky above `quranic:root_000745:B004/m01`; exposure `quranic:root_000105:B001/m01`; open ground `quranic:root_000105:B002/m01`; worshipped deity `quranic:root_000047:B001/m01`; divine unity `quranic:root_001631:B004/m01`; overpowering from above `quranic:root_001266:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:48: تُبَدَّلُ، الْأَرْضُ، غَيْرَ، السَّمَاوَاتُ، بَرَزُوا، لِلَّهِ، الْوَاحِدِ، الْقَهَّارِ.
- Synthesis: Judgment first changes the stage. Substitution and alteration re-form the environment; emergence onto open ground removes cover; unity and overpowering eliminate every competing center of authority.

#### Subchannel B. Paired Bodies in Fettered Conjunction
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Criminal bodies are visibly fastened into pairs or cohorts by binding restraints.
- Active motifs: seeing `quranic:root_000531:B001/m01`; crime `quranic:root_000239:B004/m01`; bodily mass `quranic:root_000239:B007/m01`; binding `quranic:root_001221:B001/m01`; paired companion `quranic:root_001221:B002/m01`; coeval group `quranic:root_001221:B005/m01`; conjunction of soul and body `quranic:root_001221:B009/m01`; fetter `quranic:root_000868:B002/m01`; totality `quranic:root_001315:B003/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:49, 51: تَرَى، الْمُجْرِمِينَ، مُقَرَّنِينَ، الْأَصْفَادِ، كُلَّ، نَفْسٍ، كَسَبَتْ.
- Synthesis: Conjunction is the operative image: individuals are made companions through physical binding, while body, soul, and acquired crime are brought into one visible relation. Association now takes the form of inescapable fitted accountability.

#### Subchannel C. Tar Garments and Enveloping Fire
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Flowing tar is fitted as clothing, while fire covers faces and alters flesh through heat.
- Active motifs: garment `quranic:root_000692:B001/m01`; flowing tar `quranic:root_001238:B005/m01`; dripping or flowing material `quranic:root_001238:B003/m01`; covering `quranic:root_001088:B001/m01`; complete envelopment `quranic:root_001088:B002/m01`; face `quranic:root_001630:B001/m01`; fire `quranic:root_001564:B002/m01`; baking or scorching heat `quranic:root_001122:B001/m01`; flesh altered by fire `quranic:root_001266:B002/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:43, 48, 50: أَفْئِدَتُهُمْ، الْقَهَّارِ، سَرَابِيلُهُمْ، قَطِرَانٍ، تَغْشَى، وُجُوهَهُمُ، النَّارُ.
- Synthesis: Punishment is fitted at two scales: tar adheres as the body's garment, and fire extends over the face from outside. Flow, cover, heat, and altered flesh turn clothing from protection into a material that sustains exposure.

#### Subchannel D. Equivalent Repayment and Rapid Account
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Every self receives an equivalent settlement for what it acquired, and the account is closed without delay.
- Active motifs: equivalent repayment `quranic:root_000244:B001/m01`; settling a debt `quranic:root_000244:B003/m01`; worshipped deity `quranic:root_000047:B001/m01`; totality `quranic:root_001315:B003/m01`; self `quranic:root_001533:B012/m01`; acquired gain `quranic:root_001296:B001/m01`; acquisition `quranic:root_000239:B003/m01`; speed `quranic:root_000698:B001/m01`; account and calculation `quranic:root_000318:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:49, 51: الْمُجْرِمِينَ، لِيَجْزِيَ اللَّهُ، كُلَّ نَفْسٍ، مَا كَسَبَتْ، سَرِيعُ الْحِسَابِ.
- Synthesis: Recompense is rendered as exact financial settlement rather than undifferentiated penalty. Each self's acquisition fixes the equivalent due, while speed characterizes completion of the account rather than approximation.

### 13. P4 (14:42-52): Message Reaches Its Cognitive End
- Semantic invariant: Communication is complete only when public delivery produces warning, distinguishing knowledge, unification, and recollection in the inner intellect.
- Surface relation: direct; 14:52: هَذَا بَلَاغٌ لِلنَّاسِ، لِيُنْذَرُوا بِهِ، لِيَعْلَمُوا، إِلَهٌ وَاحِدٌ، لِيَذَّكَّرَ أُولُو الْأَلْبَابِ.
- Surprising reach: “Reaching” joins destination and eloquence, while the intellect is pictured as an inner core capable of recovering what had been forgotten.

#### Subchannel A. Delivery That Reaches Its Audience
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A public communication reaches its intended destination and becomes an effective warning.
- Active motifs: reaching a goal `quranic:root_000151:B001/m01`; delivering a message `quranic:root_000151:B002/m01`; eloquence reaching intended meaning `quranic:root_000151:B004/m01`; people as audience `quranic:root_000059:B001/m01`; warning `quranic:root_001488:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:52: بَلَاغٌ، لِلنَّاسِ، لِيُنْذَرُوا، بِهِ.
- Synthesis: Delivery is measured by arrival, not emission. The same root binds destination, message-bearing, and eloquence, so warning succeeds when wording and audience meet at the intended cognitive point.

#### Subchannel B. Distinguishing Knowledge and Recollection
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The received message unveils a distinguishing sign, establishes singular deity, and prompts the inner core to recover knowledge.
- Active motifs: unveiled knowledge `quranic:root_001040:B001/m01`; distinguishing sign `quranic:root_001040:B002/m01`; worshipped deity `quranic:root_000047:B001/m01`; divine unity `quranic:root_001631:B004/m01`; recall after forgetting `quranic:root_000516:B003/m01`; reminder `quranic:root_000516:B009/m01`; pure intellect `quranic:root_001338:B004/m01`; inner core `quranic:root_001338:B003/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:52: لِيَعْلَمُوا، إِلَهٌ، وَاحِدٌ، لِيَذَّكَّرَ، أُولُو الْأَلْبَابِ.
- Synthesis: Knowledge here is discriminating recognition: the sign separates one divine center from alternatives. Recollection then reaches the “core,” presenting understanding as recovered interior orientation rather than newly stored information alone.

## Standalone Subchannels

### S1. P1 (14:1-15): Gratitude Becomes Visible Yield
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Recognition of an allotted good becomes visible as sufficiency, fullness, new growth, and increase; concealment interrupts that yield.
- Active motifs: blessing and good condition `quranic:root_001525:B001/m01`; recognizing blessing `quranic:root_000810:B001/m01`; a little proving sufficient and leaving a visible effect `quranic:root_000810:B002/m01`; fullness like abundant milk `quranic:root_000810:B003/m01`; new shoot or sprout `quranic:root_000810:B004/m01`; growth and increase `quranic:root_000657:B001/m01`; covering a blessing `quranic:root_001307:B004/m01`; praise `quranic:root_000355:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:5-8: شَكُورٍ، نِعْمَةَ، شَكَرْتُمْ، لَأَزِيدَنَّكُمْ، كَفَرْتُمْ، غَنِيٌّ، حَمِيدٌ.
- Synthesis: Gratitude is activated as a productive response rather than a verbal label. Recognition makes the gift show in sufficiency, fullness, and fresh growth; increase extends that yield, while covering the blessing blocks its visibility. This formed process remains local but does not require the coercion or messenger scenes as a parent.

### S2. Whole-surah: A Written Contract of Release
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: An owner and an owned servant execute a written manumission compact whose staged obligation is dictated, recorded, and given a document title.
- Active motifs: owned servant `quranic:root_000973:B001/m01`; owning hand `quranic:root_001693:B004/m01`; manumission contract with scheduled payments `quranic:root_001283:B005/m01`; dictation to a writer `quranic:root_001445:B004/m01`; document title `quranic:root_001041:B002/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:1, 9-11, 13, 31, 35, 38: كِتَابٌ، أَيْدِيَهُمْ، يَعْبُدُ، عِبَادِهِ، مِلَّتِنَا، عِبَادِيَ، عَلَانِيَةً، نَعْبُدَ، نُعْلِنُ.
- Synthesis: Ownership, writing, dictation, instalments, and titling form one legal passage from servitude toward freedom. This materializes the surah's primary movement from darkness and subjection into light as document-mediated release: a binding word opens freedom through an acknowledged relation and fulfilled obligation, not through self-authorized escape.

### S3. Whole-surah: Livestock Brought to the Sanctuary Blade
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Livestock designated as a gift is brought within a sanctuary boundary, constituted as an offering for nearness, and met at the sacrificial station by the throat-blade that stills the victim.
- Active motifs: livestock wealth, especially camels `quranic:root_001525:B005/m01`; gift driven to the sanctuary `quranic:root_001583:B005/m01`; sanctuary precinct `quranic:root_000313:B003/m01`; offering made for nearness to God `quranic:root_001212:B005/m01`; throat slaughter `quranic:root_000508:B001/m01`; sacrificial knife `quranic:root_000508:B003/m01`; altar or sacrificial station `quranic:root_000508:B003/m02`; blade that stills the victim `quranic:root_000726:B007/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:6, 12, 14, 28, 34, 37, 44-45: نِعْمَةَ، يُذَبِّحُونَ، هَدَانَا، لَنُسْكِنَنَّكُمْ، نِعْمَتَ، أَسْكَنْتُ، الْمُحَرَّمِ، قَرِيبٍ، سَكَنْتُمْ، مَسَاكِنِ.
- Synthesis: Animal, destination, offering relation, station, tool, bodily operation, and stillness complete a directed sacrificial rite. This supports and materializes Ibrahim's sanctuary sequence as enacted nearness, while usefully pressuring the primary account of 14:6: coerced child-slaughter shares the throat and blade but lacks the gift relation, protected destination, and devotional end of an offering.

### S4. Whole-surah: The Pot Lowered from the Fire
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Flesh cooks in a pot until fire draws out its liquid and weakens it, while a dedicated cloth lowers the hot vessel and protects the handler.
- Active motifs: cooking pot `quranic:root_001205:B007/m01`; cloth for lowering a pot from fire `quranic:root_000248:B007/m01`; roasting or cooking heat `quranic:root_000879:B001/m01`; flesh changed and made to run with liquid under fire `quranic:root_001266:B002/m01`; strength or sharpness deadened by cooking `quranic:root_001454:B002/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:17-18, 30-31, 35, 37, 40, 48: الْمَوْتُ، مَيِّتٍ، يَقْدِرُونَ، جَعَلُوا، الصَّلَاةَ، اجْعَلْ، الْقَهَّارِ.
- Synthesis: Vessel, flesh, heat, rendered liquid, weakening, and heat-cloth form a complete cooking mechanism. This materializes the surah's primary fire judgment as processing that changes flesh, and the protective cloth usefully pressures the tar-garment image: what normally separates a hand from heat is reversed into clothing that binds the body to fire.

### S5. Whole-surah: Lot-Arrows in a Wager
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Gaming arrows are held in a leather case, the numbered fifth lot participates in a wager, and victory by deception deprives the rival of the share he expected.
- Active motifs: leather case holding gaming arrows `quranic:root_000532:B010/m01`; fifth gaming arrow or lot `quranic:root_001533:B016/m01`; wagering contest won by deception `quranic:root_001255:B007/m01`; gambling victory that withholds an expected share `quranic:root_000313:B007/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:18, 22, 33, 37, 45, 51: رَبَّهُمْ، أَنْفُسَكُمْ، الْقَمَرَ، الْمُحَرَّمِ، أَنْفُسِهِمْ، نَفْسٍ.
- Synthesis: Container, numbered lot, contest, deceptive victory, and lost share form a compact gaming scene. This usefully pressures the surah's primary exchange-and-account reading: false promise and worldly gain resemble a wager whose apparatus stages order while permitting deception, whereas final recompense settles each self's acquisition exactly rather than by chance.

### S6. Whole-surah: Milk Retained, Drawn, and Renewed
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: An udder fills, careful fingertip milking draws a small flow, a retained trace prompts the next milk, the udder replenishes between milkings, and continued yield is worked until butter appears.
- Active motifs: udder filling and gathering milk `quranic:root_000555:B007/m01`; fingertip milking and its small flow `quranic:root_001165:B005/m01`; retained milk that calls forth the next flow `quranic:root_000478:B003/m01`; milk returning between milkings `quranic:root_001188:B006/m01`; continuous milk yield `quranic:root_000563:B006/m01`; milk gathering and producing butter in the churn `quranic:root_000205:B004/m01`; abundant full milk after pasture `quranic:root_000810:B003/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:4-5, 7, 9-11, 13, 22, 26, 32, 37, 39-40, 43-44, 47: أَرْسَلْنَا، رَسُولٍ، شَكُورٍ، شَكَرْتُمْ، فَرَدُّوا، تَدْعُونَنَا، فَاطِرِ، دَعَوْتُكُمْ، فَوْقِ، الثَّمَرَاتِ، يَشْكُرُونَ، الدُّعَاءِ، يَرْتَدُّ، الرُّسُلَ.
- Synthesis: Fullness, measured extraction, retained starter, replenishment, continuing flow, and transformation into butter make provision a managed cycle rather than a static possession. This materializes the surah's primary provision-and-gratitude reading: a gift becomes fruitful through careful receipt, restraint, return, and renewed yield rather than one-time seizure.

### S7. Whole-surah: Perfume Pounded and Applied
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A fragrant substance is pounded on a broad slab with a hard smooth crushing stone, then applied to another surface as an aromatic coating.
- Active motifs: fragrant substance used as perfume `quranic:root_000961:B007/m01`; broad stone slab for pounding perfume `quranic:root_000879:B008/m01`; hard smooth crushing stone `quranic:root_001266:B004/m01`; preparation and application of an aromatic coating `quranic:root_000434:B010/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:19, 24, 31-32, 37, 40, 48: خَلَقَ، كَلِمَةً طَيِّبَةً، الصَّلَاةَ، الْقَهَّارِ.
- Synthesis: Aromatic material, pounding bed, crushing tool, preparation, and coating form a complete perfume craft. This reframes the primary good-word image of 14:24 as crafted fragrance: its goodness is not merely an abstract quality but a prepared effect that passes beyond its small material source and adheres to what receives it.

### S8. Whole-surah: Kohl Prepared and Turned in the Eye
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Soot pigment is prepared as kohl on a mirror, taken up on a slender applicator, turned within the seeing eye, and ordinarily released through eyelid movement and a returning gaze.
- Active motifs: wick or fat soot used as kohl `quranic:root_001564:B008/m01`; kohl material rubbed on a mirror `quranic:root_000848:B013/m01`; applicator rod turned in the eye `quranic:root_001445:B007/m01`; seeing eye `quranic:root_000121:B001/m01`; blink and edge of gaze `quranic:root_000931:B002/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:1, 3, 5, 10, 13, 16, 30, 42-43, 50: النُّورِ، يَصُدُّونَ، تَصُدُّونَنَا، مِلَّتِنَا، صَدِيدٍ، النَّارِ، الْأَبْصَارُ، طَرْفُهُمْ.
- Synthesis: Pigment, preparation surface, applicator, eye, and blink form a controlled sequence of bodily care. This usefully pressures the primary judgment scene of 14:42-43: an eye normally receives a measured stroke and regains control through lid and gaze, but terror arrests the eyes and denies the returning glance.

### S9. Whole-surah: A Smelted and Gilded Cult Image
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Metal is melted and refined as bellows expel its dross; a copper cult body is then made to present a more precious surface through gold plating.
- Active motifs: molten copper `quranic:root_001238:B006/m01`; smelting dross expelled by bellows `quranic:root_000386:B002/m01`; gold metal `quranic:root_000522:B001/m01`; gilding with gold `quranic:root_000522:B002/m01`; plating base metal to present a false precious surface `quranic:root_001458:B005/m01`; idol body made from wood, silver, or copper `quranic:root_000886:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:16, 19, 26, 32, 35, 50: مَاءٍ، يُذْهِبْكُمْ، خَبِيثَةٍ، مَاءً، الْأَصْنَامَ، قَطِرَانٍ.
- Synthesis: Melted metal, expelled waste, idol body, precious metal, and deceptive coating form one manufacturing sequence. This materializes and reframes the primary warning against idols and substituted rivals: their apparent preciousness is engineered at the surface, concealing the base composition beneath the object offered as divine.

### S10. P1→P2→P3→P4: A Guided Procession Becomes Compulsory Rush
- Reading type: mixed
- Pericopes and bridge: P1 (14:1-15) → P2 (14:16-27) → P3 (14:28-41) → P4 (14:42-52); role progression and reversal. A guide establishes the front and pace, followers trace and close the distance, and chosen following becomes urgent forward motion.
- Scene or process: Road users move behind a guide, follow traces one by one, catch the one ahead, and continue as a procession whose easy gait is finally reversed into rapid, plunging travel.
- Active motifs: road users and travelers `quranic:root_000672:B002/m01`; guide or leading front `quranic:root_001583:B003/m01`; catching the one ahead `quranic:root_000175:B002/m01`; tracking trace after trace `quranic:root_000175:B003/m01`; uninterrupted following sequence `quranic:root_000175:B004/m01`; easy gait `quranic:root_000563:B003/m01`; rapid or plunging travel `quranic:root_001609:B008/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:3-4, 9-13, 21, 30, 36-37, 43-44, 47: سَبِيلِ، يَهْدِي، أَرْسَلْنَا، رَسُولٍ، سُبُلَنَا، تَبَعًا، سَبِيلِ، تَبِعَنِي، تَهْوِي، هَوَاءٌ، نَتَّبِعِ، الرُّسُلَ.
- Synthesis: P1 supplies road, guide, and measured dispatch; P2 places followers behind leaders; P3 makes the identity of the followed guide decisive; P4 records the late wish to follow and converts controlled movement into haste without recovered agency. The bridge reframes guidance as pace and relational order as well as direction: failure to follow while choice remains becomes compulsory advance when the destination can no longer be changed.

### S11. P1→P2→P3→P4: A Right Carried from Deed to Restitution
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Pericopes and bridge: P1 (14:1-15) → P2 (14:16-27) → P3 (14:28-41) → P4 (14:42-52); repeated legal scene signature and causal sequence. A specific entitlement persists through deed, claimant, proof, adjudication, collection, and return.
- Scene or process: The holder of a documented right advances a claim, establishes it by proof before a judge, collects what is due, and receives the right back.
- Active motifs: claimant pursuing a right `quranic:root_000175:B005/m01`; specifically owned right `quranic:root_000347:B003/m01`; deed or certificate of right `quranic:root_000516:B008/m01`; proof establishing the claim `quranic:root_000192:B006/m01`; judgment between people `quranic:root_000348:B002/m01`; payment and collection of the right `quranic:root_001237:B006/m01`; return of the right to its owner `quranic:root_000609:B006/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:4-6, 18-19, 21-22, 24-25, 27, 36, 44, 52: حَكِيمٌ، ذَكِّرْهُمْ، اذْكُرُوا، الرِّيحُ، بِالْحَقِّ، تَبَعًا، قُضِيَ، ثَابِتٌ، يَتَذَكَّرُ، يُثَبِّتُ، تَبِعَنِي، نَتَّبِعِ، لِيَذَّكَّرَ.
- Synthesis: The shared invariant is preservation of entitlement while participants and stages change: a right remains identifiable from written deed through proof and judgment to satisfaction. This causal bridge reframes the surah's final account as restitution rather than sudden penalty; delay does not dissolve the claim because its deed, proof, and claimant continue toward return.

### S12. P2→P3→P4: Stipulated Wage Reversed into Corvee
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Pericopes and bridge: P2 (14:16-27) → P3 (14:28-41) → P4 (14:42-52); contrast and role reversal. Work can transfer an assigned gain to its performer, or authority can extract the same capacity without wage.
- Scene or process: Workers perform a task for an assigned wage that causes gain to accrue to them, while the opposed labor regime compels service without compensation.
- Active motifs: manual workers or craftsmen `quranic:root_001167:B003/m01`; worker's wage or livelihood `quranic:root_001046:B004/m01`; reward stipulated for a task `quranic:root_000248:B005/m01`; causing another to earn gain `quranic:root_001296:B002/m01`; compelled labor without wage `quranic:root_000685:B002/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:18, 23, 27, 30, 32-33, 35, 37, 40, 42, 45, 51: أَعْمَالُهُمْ، كَسَبُوا، عَمِلُوا، يَفْعَلُ، جَعَلُوا، سَخَّرَ، اجْعَلْ، يَعْمَلُ، فَعَلْنَا، كَسَبَتْ.
- Synthesis: The bridge is an exact contrast in labor relation, not in activity: one arrangement names the worker, fixes compensation, and transfers gain, while the other takes work without wage. This usefully pressures P3's harnessed cosmos as service ordered toward provision rather than exploitative corvee, while P4 restores the question of what is due for every performed act.

### S13. P1→P2→P3→P4: Income Entered, Assessed, and Paid Out
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Pericopes and bridge: P1 (14:1-15) → P2 (14:16-27) → P3 (14:28-41) → P4 (14:42-52); causal sequence. Gain enters an estate, an obligation is assessed, a fixed amount exits, and payment discharges the charge.
- Scene or process: Income is received into a holding, a levy is imposed on person, property, or land, money leaves along its specified channel, and the assessed tribute is paid.
- Active motifs: income entering an estate `quranic:root_000464:B006/m01`; levy imposed on a person or property `quranic:root_000906:B010/m01`; money exiting as a fixed obligation `quranic:root_000400:B003/m01`; tribute or tax delivered `quranic:root_000009:B008/m01`; financial payment discharging what is due `quranic:root_000244:B004/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:1, 5, 9-11, 13, 17, 19, 23-25, 31-32, 34, 44-45, 51: تُخْرِجَ، أَخْرِجْ، يَأْتِ، نُخْرِجَ، أُدْخِلَ، ضَرَبَ، تُؤْتِي، أَخْرَجَ، آتَاكُمْ، يَأْتِي، ضَرَبْنَا، لِيَجْزِيَ.
- Synthesis: Receipt, assessment, outflow, and satisfaction form one complete revenue circuit across the surah. This causal bridge materializes the movement from bestowed resources to exact account: what enters possession remains answerable to an assigned due, while freely circulating expenditure and imposed levy differ in agency even though both send wealth outward.

### S14. P3 (14:28-41): The Bargained Sale and Its Rescission
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A food seller displays merchandise, the parties bargain and state its value, an advance is paid, they transact, and the sale may be unwound by returning the goods.
- Active motifs: food seller `quranic:root_000095:B005/m01`; merchandise offered for sale `quranic:root_000169:B002/m01`; bargaining and stating a price `quranic:root_000764:B001/m01`; valuation and pricing `quranic:root_001273:B010/m01`; advance payment in a sale `quranic:root_000737:B005/m01`; transaction between parties `quranic:root_001046:B005/m01`; return and rescission of a sale `quranic:root_000555:B004/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:6, 9, 18, 23, 28, 31, 40-43, 48: يَسُومُونَكُمْ، رَدُّوا، أَعْمَالُهُمْ، سَلَامٌ، عَمِلُوا، بَدَّلُوا، بَيْعٌ، يُقِيمُوا، مُقِيمَ، يَقُومُ، يَعْمَلُ، يَرْتَدُّ، تُبَدَّلُ.
- Synthesis: Offer, negotiation, valuation, advance, completed dealing, and rescission form the full life cycle of a sale. This materializes the primary warning in 14:31: the coming day without sale closes not just payment but every market operation by which people normally renegotiate value, transfer goods, or reverse a bad bargain.

### S15. P3 (14:28-41): Food Passed as a Gift, Then Recalled
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Food is heaped on a cloth and palm-strip plate, sent as a gift to a friend, passed hand to hand, and later converted into a burden when the giver recounts the favor.
- Active motifs: tablecloth and heaped food `quranic:root_000840:B011/m01`; palm-strip plate for food or gifts `quranic:root_001263:B006/m01`; gift sent to a loved person `quranic:root_001583:B004/m01`; hand-to-hand passing `quranic:root_001693:B007/m01`; giver burdening the recipient by recalling a favor `quranic:root_001449:B003/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:4-5, 9, 11-12, 21, 43: يَهْدِي، صَبَّارٍ، أَيْدِيَهُمْ، يَمُنُّ، نَصْبِرَ، هَدَانَا، صَبَرْنَا، هَدَى، مُقْنِعِي.
- Synthesis: Food, serving surface, gift vessel, affectionate destination, manual transfer, and retrospective speech complete a hospitality scene. It materializes P3's provision and expenditure as goods made shareable, while the recalled favor usefully pressures the surah's gratitude reading: benefaction becomes domination when the giver uses memory of the gift to weigh down its recipient.

### S16. P2→P3: The Full Bucket from a Permanent Well
- Reading type: mixed
- Pericopes and bridge: P2 (14:16-27) → P3 (14:28-41); contrast in source, handling, and reception. P2 forces a foul draught that cannot be swallowed, while P3 supplies water within a created provision system.
- Scene or process: A permanent well in grazing land yields a full bucket balanced by its crosspiece; the drawn water fills a waterskin and reaches the drinker as sweet and palatable.
- Active motifs: well in grazing land `quranic:root_000227:B010/m01`; permanent well whose water does not cease `quranic:root_000989:B004/m01`; large full bucket `quranic:root_000521:B007/m01`; bucket crosspiece balancing the load `quranic:root_000741:B008/m01`; waterskin for drawn water `quranic:root_001212:B009/m01`; sweet palatable water `quranic:root_000994:B001/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:10, 17, 19, 34, 39, 44: ذُنُوبِكُمْ، عَذَابٌ، جَدِيدٍ، تَعُدُّوا، سَمِيعُ، قَرِيبٍ.
- Synthesis: Water source, persistence, lifting vessel, load-balancing component, receiving skin, and drinkable outcome form one extraction sequence. This materializes P3's provision as accessible through a fitted chain of source and tools; by contrast, P2's liquid reaches the mouth under compulsion yet never becomes a received, sustaining drink.

### S17. P3 (14:28-41): Stored Water Released to Harvest
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Well, canal, or rainwater collects in a broad reservoir and rock hollow, then issues through an outlet into fields until the irrigated crop reaches harvest.
- Active motifs: reservoir that gathers water and releases it to crops `quranic:root_000016:B007/m01`; rock hollow retaining rainwater `quranic:root_000434:B011/m01`; water issuing from an outlet into fields `quranic:root_001124:B005/m01`; river mouth or outlet `quranic:root_001190:B004/m01`; harvested crop `quranic:root_000630:B006/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:9-10, 15, 19, 32, 37, 44: أَفْوَاهِهِمْ، أَجَلٍ، اسْتَفْتَحُوا، خَلَقَ، خَلْقٍ، زَرْعٍ، قَرِيبٍ.
- Synthesis: Collection, retention, controlled release, field entry, and harvest form a complete irrigation mechanism. It materializes P3's primary sky-water-fruit sequence by supplying the middle operations between descent and yield, and sharpens the barren-valley petition of 14:37 as a request for a missing storage-and-release pathway rather than for undifferentiated abundance.

### S18. P2→P4: The Dead Carried, Shrouded, and Housed
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Pericopes and bridge: P2 (14:16-27) → P4 (14:42-52); interrupted sequence and reversal. Ordinary death proceeds from body to concealment and grave, but P2 withholds completion and P4 replaces concealment with exposure.
- Scene or process: A human body is placed on a bier, carried, wrapped and concealed, and deposited in a grave understood as the dead person's house.
- Active motifs: human body lying or raised `quranic:root_000221:B002/m01`; funeral bier or carrier `quranic:root_000067:B008/m01`; shroud covering the dead `quranic:root_000266:B009/m01`; burial concealing the dead `quranic:root_000266:B009/m02`; grave as the dead person's house `quranic:root_000166:B007/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:6, 23, 26, 37, 52: آلِ، جَنَّاتٍ، اجْتُثَّتْ، بَيْتِكَ، أُولُو الْأَلْبَابِ.
- Synthesis: Body, carrier, shroud, concealment, and grave-house complete the ordinary terminal passage. This usefully pressures P2's death that approaches from every side without allowing death: the expected sequence cannot discharge into burial. P4 then reverses the grave's housing function as the transformed earth and universal appearance before God remove final concealment.

### S19. P4 (14:42-52): The Moonlit Snare and the Checked Prey
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Hunters go out at moonlight to seek prey whose sight is impaired, set snares with dangling cords, catch the quarry, and inspect it to determine whether the strike killed it.
- Active motifs: hunters going out for game `quranic:root_000745:B006/m01`; seeking prey by moonlight when its sight is impaired `quranic:root_001255:B003/m01`; hunter's snare `quranic:root_000791:B006/m01`; traps set for prey `quranic:root_000879:B004/m01`; dangling cord of a snare `quranic:root_000994:B006/m01`; checking whether struck prey is dead `quranic:root_001454:B014/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:17, 22, 31, 33, 37, 40, 44, 48: الْمَوْتُ، أَشْرَكْتُمُونِ، الصَّلَاةَ، الْقَمَرَ، مُقِيمِي الصَّلَاةِ، عَذَابُ، السَّمَاوَاتُ.
- Synthesis: Nocturnal setting, impaired quarry, hunter, laid apparatus, catching cord, and verification form a complete capture scene. This materializes the primary plotting of 14:46 as staged entrapment rather than thought alone: concealment and compromised sight let the apparatus do its work, while the final inspection anticipates judgment's disclosure of what the scheme actually accomplished.

### S20. P2 (14:16-27): Termites Inside the Load-Bearing Frame
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Wood-eating insects consume a crosswise beam within an interlocked wooden frame until corrosion reaches the structure's supporting members.
- Active motifs: termite or wood-eating insect `quranic:root_000025:B010/m01`; crosswise load-bearing beam `quranic:root_000243:B007/m01`; interlocked wooden support frame `quranic:root_000777:B004/m01`; corrosion and eating-away `quranic:root_000043:B006/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:19, 21, 24-26, 32, 48: الْأَرْضَ، جَزِعْنَا، شَجَرَةٍ، أُكُلَهَا، اجْتُثَّتْ، الْأَرْضُ.
- Synthesis: Pest, material, beam, frame, and progressive decay form a hidden structural-failure mechanism. This materializes P2's bad tree with no stability: visible members may remain aligned while their carrying substance is eaten away, so eventual uprooting exposes an earlier loss of internal support rather than a merely sudden fall.

### S21. P1→P3: The Hidden Fetus Received into the Midwife's Hand
- Reading type: mixed
- Pericopes and bridge: P1 (14:1-15) → P3 (14:28-41); role progression and violent reversal. P3's gifted lineage passes through protected gestation and reception, while P1 destroys children after that care sequence has produced them.
- Scene or process: The womb houses a hidden fetus, labor draws near, a midwife receives the emerging child into her hand, birth completes, and the umbilical remnant is cut.
- Active motifs: womb as the child's seedbed and house `quranic:root_000552:B003/m01`; fetus hidden in the womb `quranic:root_000266:B007/m01`; labor drawing near `quranic:root_001212:B012/m01`; midwife receiving the newborn into her hand `quranic:root_001198:B007/m01`; birth and placing down the pregnancy `quranic:root_001683:B003/m01`; infant navel and cut umbilical remnant `quranic:root_000697:B006/m01`.
- Ayah anchors: 14:9, 22-23, 31, 36, 40-41, 44: قَبْلِهِمْ، قَبْلُ، جَنَّاتٍ، سِرًّا، رَحِيمٌ، تَقَبَّلْ، وَالِدَيَّ، قَرِيبٍ.
- Synthesis: Gestational place, concealed child, approaching labor, receiving specialist, delivery, and bodily separation form a complete birth sequence. It supports and materializes P3's account of offspring as a received gift, while usefully pressuring P1's child-slaughter as the violent cancellation of a prior womb-to-hand process of protection, emergence, and care.
